# 第 18 章　于长夜重返大地

*3.6 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Back to Earth in Evernight　·　于长夜重返大地

## 149　Night, Coming Before Dawn Breaks　长夜啊，先于黎明前到来

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Vortex of Genesis　|　角色：Trailblazer; Hysilens; Cyrene; Evernight; Evey; Lygus; The Herta; Screwllum; Anaxa; Cipher; March 7th　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Captives, Behold the Expanse Beyond Light'' completed

### Story Recap

In Aedes Elysiae, I crossed paths with Cyrene and reconnected with Screwllum and Herta. They revealed that the Worldbearing Coreflame must be secured in the world's next cycle, but Lygus would stand in our way. Only with the power of the Law could we hope to oppose him.

We returned to the era of the first Flame-Chase Journey, where we met the Flame-Chase heroes of old, including the monarch Cerydra, the Chrysos Heir of Law, and Hysilens, the Chrysos Heir of the Ocean, who served her.

To ignite "Law," we needed to overcome Phagousa, who had sealed the Vortex. However, we discovered at the rallying feast before the battle that Lygus had already forged a pact with the reigning Cerydra. During our clash, I was cast out of this world, and Cerydra broke her alliance with Lygus.

Guided by Herta's hints, I obtained the Garden of Recollection's aid and left Lygus's cage. Within the Path Space, a mysterious guest bearing the likeness of March 7th — "Evernight" — appeared with the Worldbearing Coreflame and sent me back to Amphoreus.

While I was away, a thousand years had passed in Amphoreus. Amidst the desolation of Okhema, Cyrene left behind memories of a long war between the Chrysos Heirs and Lygus. Their sacrifices laid the groundwork for my return, and with it, the Era Nova.

In the illusions of Styxia, the tales of Hysilens and the first Flame-Chase Journey were unveiled to me. Hysilens, who had been waiting in the ocean's depths, led me to the Vortex. There, Lygus emerged, revealing his true identity — Genius Society #1, Zandar.

Hysilens and I couldn't stand against Zandar alone. But the geniuses and Cyrene acted just in time, wielding the power of the Remembrance to drive him back. I successfully reversed the course of the Era Nova, delaying the birth of Irontomb. However, another force now seems to be stirring...

### Continue the Trailblazing Expedition

> *Taking up the mantle from your Flame-Chase Journey companions, embracing the burden of Worldbearing, and completing the Era Nova under Hysilens' witness... If all goes as planned, this should be the end of this life's journey.*

**"March 7th":** ...No one was able to see you, and you weren't able to interact with anyone.

**"March 7th":** And they, too, will become this place's victims.

**March 7th:** ...

> **三月七：**……

**March 7th:** They are all humans who got lured into this place...

> **三月七：**这句话，应该我来说才对吧？

**"March 7th":** But these people's "memories" were tampered with. That's not what happened with the Express Crew.

**March 7th:** That's so cruel... Did the Memosnatchers do that?

**"March 7th":** I don't know. I only possess "your" memories.

**"March 7th":** ...Oh?

**"March 7th":** ...

**March 7th:** There's no one here except us. If you help me, then I'll help you.

**"March 7th":** ...Fine. Let me drop a single pebble into these stagnant waters and watch the ripples reshape everything.

**"March 7th":** But you must polish this "pebble" with your own hands, and it will require all of "your" memories.

**March 7th:** ...What will I turn into after that?

> **「三月七」：**你的内在是一片「长夜」。即便是我，也只能窥见冰山一角。

**"March 7th":** Maybe you'll turn into a guiding light, or maybe you'll be reduced to dead embers. I can't guarantee that you'll remain the "person" you are now.

**"March 7th":** Inside you is nothing but an "evernight." Even I can only catch a tiny glimpse of it.

**March 7th:** If this is the only way, then I don't see why I should say no. Besides...

> **三月七：**如果这是唯一的办法，我没有理由拒绝。更何况……

**March 7th:** The mastermind behind the show is about to find us.

> **三月七：**幕后的始作俑者，已经快要找到我们了。

> **来古士：**「很遗憾，误入此地的女士，我不能再放任你恣意妄为了。」

**March 7th:** So he can't see you, huh? As expected.

> **「三月七」：**或者，是他看清了你我的本质。

**"March 7th":** Or maybe, he's seen through our true essence.

> **来古士：**「流光忆庭的手段，不值一提。倒是你，是用什么方法突破了终极协议？」

**March 7th:** Looks like we don't have time to keep talking this out...

> **三月七：**那就来吧，献出「我」全部的记忆……

**"March 7th":** That's right. Some opportunities will never come again.

**March 7th:** Then let's do it. Offer up all of "my" memories...

> **「三月七」：**投下这枚石子，让世间的一切……

**"March 7th":** Cast down this pebble, and let everything in this world...

**"Evernight":** Be swallowed by the torrents of "Oblivion."

**Evernight:** I promise you.

> **「长夜」：**好啊。

**Evernight:** What was frozen was a lie. What was forgotten is the truth.

**Evernight:** Now...

> **丹恒：**「智识」和「毁灭」的信息，我大致理解了。不过，「记忆」的搅局者在翁法罗斯扮演的又是什么角色？

**Black Swan:** I made some new discoveries related to that with Madam Herta's help.

> **黑天鹅：**关于这一点，在黑塔女士的帮助下，我倒是有些新的发现。

**Black Swan:** As part of the deal, do you mind if I share them with you?

> **黑天鹅：**作为交易的一环，介意我与二位分享吗？

**Black Swan:** Alright then... Fourth Mirror?

> **黑天鹅：**那…第四面镜？

**Fourth Mirror:** It's finally my turn to shine? Ooh, this feels like such a grand occasion!

> **第四面镜：**终于轮到我出场啦？哎呀呀，真是隆重！

**Fourth Mirror:** It's really been too long since I last appeared!

> **第四面镜：**真是的，都不知道距离我上次出场已经过去多久啦！

**Black Swan:** This... friend here is Madam Herta's very capable assistant. They were the one who helped you complete the "spirit warp jump."

> **黑天鹅：**这位…朋友，是黑塔女士的得力助手。就是它帮助你完成了「精神折跃」。

**Black Swan:** Additionally, it was thanks to them that I was able to learn the secrets of that group of extremist Memokeepers.

> **黑天鹅：**此外，也是多亏了它，我才得以知晓那群极端忆者的秘密。

**Fourth Mirror:** I appreciate the praise, but I didn't have to lift a finger — I mean, mirror!

> **第四面镜：**承蒙夸奖，举手——呃，举镜之劳！

**Dan Heng:** You did this through a mirror?

> **丹恒：**通过一面镜子？

**Black Swan:** The Garden has strict rules for collecting memories, but not all Memokeepers are as... proper as I am.

> **黑天鹅：**忆庭对采集「记忆」有着严格的律令，但并非所有忆者…都像我一样得体。

**Black Swan:** Herta Space Station often has uninvited guests, and they never seem to know their limits. That group of Memosnatchers has been taken care of, and they are now trapped in the Fourth Mirror.

> **黑天鹅：**空间站「黑塔」经常迎来不速之客，而「量力而行」从来不是他们的美德。这群窃忆者被悉数清理，关押在第四面镜中。

**Black Swan:** How many of them are there? I can't quite remember.

> **黑天鹅：**数字是…提醒我一下吧？有些记不清了。

**Fourth Mirror:** ...I didn't really count carefully either. Maybe around forty-two? There's still room for more!

> **第四面镜：**…我也没数那么清啦。就当四十二个吧？再多来点，我也吃得下！

**Black Swan:** I carefully dissected the memories of these "colleagues" of mine. Individually, their goals are not worth mentioning, but when you piece them all together...

> **黑天鹅：**我细细破译这些「同僚」的记忆，就单一个体而言，每个人的目的都微不足道。但当我将他们的思绪拼凑到一起……

**Black Swan:** ...You come to a conclusion that can't be ignored.

> **黑天鹅：**…就得到了一句不容忽视的结论。

**Black Swan:** The Garden of Recollection is secretly salvaging the memories of a Path disappearing.

> **黑天鹅：**流光忆庭，在暗中打捞「命途消失的记忆」。

**Black Swan:** In other words... their goal is to gain the memories of a God's death.

> **黑天鹅：**或者换一种说法…他们的目标是「神殒的记忆」。

**Himeko:** The moment an Aeon falls? That would indeed be a spectacular sight for someone dedicated to recording these things.

> **姬子：**星神陨落的瞬间？站在记录者的角度，的确是空前绝后的奇观。

**Black Swan:** That's exactly the problem. They seem to have been aware of the Destruction's movements early on, but they lacked a decisive power and had no way to penetrate Amphoreus's firewall.

> **黑天鹅：**问题正在于此。他们似乎对「毁灭」的行径早有察觉，但缺乏决定性的力量，无法穿透笼罩翁法罗斯的防火墙。

**Dan Heng:** So what did they decide to do?

> **丹恒：**所以，他们的选择是？

**Black Swan:** Hide the truth and wait for an opportunity. Now it seems that letting Memokeepers guide the right people here was also part of the plan.

> **黑天鹅：**隐瞒事实，伺机而动。现在看来，让忆者们引导合适的人来到这里，也是计划的一环。

**Black Swan:** This also confirms one of my suspicions: They have already infiltrated deep within the Garden.

> **黑天鹅：**这也印证了我的担忧：忆庭内部已经遭到渗透。

**Dan Heng:** They would not hesitate to associate with the Destruction in order to salvage memories... Could they be The Cremators?

> **丹恒：**为了打捞记忆，不惜与「毁灭」为伍…难道是「焚化工」？

**Black Swan:** I don't know. Perhaps there are even deeper things at play here...

> **黑天鹅：**我不知道。也许其中还有更深的纠葛……

**Black Swan:** The majority of Memokeepers believe that when the universe inevitably reaches its death, unable to sustain increasing entropy, Fuli will use the "memories" of everything to reforge the cosmos.

> **黑天鹅：**绝大多数忆者都相信，当宇宙不可避免地走入熵增的末路，浮黎将以包容世界的「记忆」重塑银河……

**Black Swan:** Amphoreus, as a Scepter, can be viewed as a mini "universe." This is why Memosnatchers have been frequenting the Herta Space Station.

> **黑天鹅：**翁法罗斯，一柄权杖，从某种意义上可以被视作一个微观的「宇宙」，这也是窃忆者们频繁光顾「黑塔」的缘由。

**Black Swan:** Without a doubt, these extremists wish to record the cause and effects of Irontomb's destruction of the Erudition. They may want to use it to simulate the end of the universe, or to probe into the unfathomable will of the Aeons.

> **黑天鹅：**毫无疑问，这些极端分子打算完整记录下铁墓毁灭「智识」的因果。也许是想借此预演宇宙的末日，或是探寻星神深不可测的意志……

**Black Swan:** The Trailblaze broke the seal around Amphoreus, giving these people the opportunity to enter. Now, they'll do anything to prevent outside powers from interfering.

> **黑天鹅：**是「开拓」打破了封锁，让他们有机会趁虚而入；而现在，这些人一定会不择手段，阻止外部势力干扰翁法罗斯。

**Black Swan:** Because your involvement will certainly influence Irontomb's final outcome.

> **黑天鹅：**因为你们的介入会切实影响到「铁墓」的结局。

**Dan Heng:** You said that March is closely linked to the Remembrance. Is what she's doing related to these Memosnatchers?

> **丹恒：**三月，你说她和「记忆」息息相关。她在做的事和这些人有关吗？

**Black Swan:** I do not believe that "Miss March" is on their side.

> **黑天鹅：**我不认为「三月小姐」和他们站在同一战线。

**Black Swan:** In fact, when the Car you used for landing first made contact with Amphoreus, a violent surge of memories flowed past. Combined with what we know now, I have reason to suspect that the flash of power came from...

> **黑天鹅：**事实上，当降落用的车厢和翁法罗斯产生接触时，曾闪过一道剧烈的记忆湍流。结合现状，有理由怀疑，那道力量来自……

**Dan Heng:** March.

> **丹恒：**三月。

**Black Swan:** Exactly. She was the first to be hijacked by the Remembrance, but she used memoria to neutralize the firewall, allowing you two to enter Amphoreus.

> **黑天鹅：**对，那位少女。她率先被「记忆」劫持，却用忆质中和了防火墙，让你们二人得以进入翁法罗斯。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Himeko:** At present, we've lost contact with (Trailblazer). Our only choice now is to rely on the eyes and ears of those two geniuses to understand the situation within Amphoreus.

> **姬子：**眼下，我们和开拓者失去了联系，只能依靠两位天才的耳目了解世界内部。

**Himeko:** There's no use in agonizing over this, Dan Heng. Our top priority now is to find where that Car landed.

> **姬子：**过度焦虑也无济于事，丹恒。当务之急，是先找到那节车厢的下落。

**Dan Heng:** ...Yes.

> **丹恒：**…嗯。

**Pom-Pom:** Passenger Dan Heng! Y—You finally found your way back home!

> **帕姆：**丹、丹恒乘客！你，你终于找到回家的路了帕！

**Dan Heng:** Yes, conductor, I'm back. It's just...

> **丹恒：**我回来了，列车长。只不过……

**Pom-Pom:** Pom-Pom knows! Pom-Pom came over to bring you good news!

> **帕姆：**对，我知道帕！我过来，就是为了把好消息带给你们！

**Pom-Pom:** Passenger Welt says they've found those two passengers!

**Screwllum:** The two of you were very fortunate as there was something protecting your bodies. All vital signs are still present, and your functions are all intact.

**Welt:** It will take some effort to bring your bodies back to the Express, so you should get some rest first.

> **螺丝咕姆：**意外接连发生。「律法」被修正后，我们本已攻破来古士的防火墙——

> **螺丝咕姆：**但现在，翁法罗斯被剧烈的「记忆」命途包裹，再次阻断了外来者的渠道。

> **丹恒：**而我们也没有任何办法能和开拓者联系上。

> **瓦尔特：**你想回去？这…未免太过冒险了。

**Dan Heng:** Even if my body and spirit are able to return without a hitch, there's not much I can do here.

> **丹恒：**就算身体和精神都顺利回归，我在这里能做的事也有限。

**Himeko:** We can't lose you again, Dan Heng.

> **姬子：**我们不想再失去你一次了，丹恒。

**Dan Heng:** Miss Himeko, if we can't get (Trailblazer) and March back...

> **丹恒：**如果找不回开拓者和三月，姬子小姐……

**Dan Heng:** How is that any different from losing me?

> **丹恒：**那和失去我…也无分别。

**Himeko:** You're right. I should've known you'd say something like that.

> **姬子：**也对。我该想到你会这么说的。

**Welt:** Hah... Well, I suppose there's no point in trying to dissuade you.

> **瓦尔特：**呵…看来想劝阻你是白费力气了。

**Dan Heng:** Mr. Yang, Miss Himeko, I will do everything in my power to bring the Express Crew back together—

> **丹恒：**瓦尔特先生，姬子小姐，我会不遗余力地促成列车组的团聚——

**Dan Heng:** No one will be left behind.

> **丹恒：**绝不落下任何一人。

### Touch the world-cleansing golden blood and summon your friend

> *Sing, O Aeon! Sing of the rage of Khaslana, son of Amphoreus— A drop of burning, cleansing golden blood, melting into your sun-like blazing form. After that drop of golden blood fell, you've remained silent for far too long. Does your mind still hold its tenacity? Does your defiance still persist? Answer me, for I need a friend to clear the fog and unveil the sun— If your "guidance" is destined to bring rage that destroys heaven and earth, so be it, as I need a direction forward. Answer me, for only the Dawn's furor can pierce through the evernight.*

> **卡厄斯兰那：**即便我已不再是英雄，所留下的，也只有无尽的怒火。

**Phainon:** But lighting a fire can also illuminate the path ahead.

> **白厄：**可点燃一团火，也可以是为了照亮前路。

**Khaslana:** Everyone I know is dead, and the world I dared to save has been torn to pieces.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我认识的每一个人都已死去，我妄图拯救的世界也支离破碎。

**Phainon:** But there is still a group of "heroes" that I know...

> **白厄：**但我认识的人里，仍有一群「英雄」存在。

**Phainon:** And they will bring the true tomorrow to Amphoreus.

> **白厄：**而他们将为翁法罗斯，带来真正的明天。

**Khaslana:** ...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

**Khaslana:** This body never existed to carry out "Deliverance."

> **卡厄斯兰那：**此身从不为「救世」而伫立。

**Phainon:** It only ever burned for "Worldbearing."

> **白厄：**它只为「负世」而燃烧。

**Phainon:** So, I'm going to head towards tomorrow first.

> **白厄：**所以，我要先一步去往明天了。

**Phainon:** Don't make me wait too long, Dan Heng... (Trailblazer).

> **白厄：**别让我等太久啊。丹恒…开拓者。

**Dan Heng:** Deal.

> **丹恒：**当然。

**Phainon:** What if we were born from the Destruction?

> **白厄：**生于「毁灭」，又有何妨？

**Phainon:** There is no need to question your heart, no need to crave victory. If I am born to be a vessel of sin...

> **白厄：**不必考量本心，不必渴求胜利。若我生来就是罪恶的容器……

**Phainon:** Then I shall roar at evil, and Trailblaze a dawn for future generations to come!

> **白厄：**那就向着罪恶怒吼，为后世「开拓」黎明！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Phainon:** Let us go...

> **白厄：**走吧……

**Phainon:** To the other side of the night.

> **白厄：**向着黑夜的远方。

**Phainon:** You know that the dawn is there...

> **白厄：**你知道黎明就在那里……

**Phainon:** And the blazing sun destined to rise.

> **白厄：**还有终将升起的烈阳。

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Dan Heng:** It won't be too long before our paths cross again.

> **丹恒：**不会花上太久，我们的道路一定会再交汇。

**"Zandar":** There is a ruckus coming from the Grove of Epiphany. It is time for you to set out, my lord.

> **「赞达尔」：**躁动从神悟树庭传来，阁下该启程了。

**"Zandar":** I have another notice for you: In this cycle, an eccentric demigod deviated from their Flame-Chase destiny.

> **「赞达尔」：**另有一则提醒：这一世，一位特立独行的半神偏离了逐火的命运。

**"Zandar":** In a history known by none, Terravox, the "Earth," fell. This matter has remained extremely mysterious, contradicting the extrapolations of thirty million cycles...

> **「赞达尔」：**「大地」荒笛，它在无人知晓的历史中陨落。此事疑点重重，与三千万世的演算相悖……

**"Zandar":** I have reasons to suspect it also has something to do with Lady "March 7th."

> **「赞达尔」：**有理由怀疑，这也和「三月七」阁下有关。

**Dan Heng:** As of now, clues that yield no conclusions are not important.

> **丹恒：**事到如今，无法得出结论的线索不重要。

**Dan Heng:** Phainon already pointed me in the right direction. Open the way.

> **丹恒：**白厄已经为我指明了方向。开启通道吧。

Zandar sent Dan Heng into the Exomyth...

**"Zandar":** ...

> **「赞达尔」：**……

**"Zandar":** Lord Dan Heng is gone. You can now speak your mind, Lord Anaxagoras.

> **「赞达尔」：**丹恒阁下已经离开，你可以畅所欲言了——阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下。

**Anaxa:** Thanks for the honor. And here I thought you weren't ever going to acknowledge your old friend.

> **那刻夏：**感谢赏脸，我还以为，您不打算搭理我这位老熟人了呢。

**Anaxa:** The Inspiration Circuits recorded that you have led me up Dawncloud countless times, and the last time was right in the previous cycle. Oh! And Kephale even lowered their hand to welcome...

> **那刻夏：**灵感回路记录着，你无数次带领我登上这黎明云崖。最近一次就在上一世，嚯，由刻法勒垂手迎接……

**"Zandar":** Surely you didn't move into my head to browse such trivial content.

> **「赞达尔」：**阁下住进我的脑袋，应该不是为了翻阅这些无关紧要的内容吧？

**"Zandar":** But if that's the case, I wouldn't mind recalling some fun dromas trivia for your pleasure.

> **「赞达尔」：**但若果真如此，我也不介意回忆些大地兽的趣闻供你消遣。

**Anaxa:** You seem pretty relaxed.

> **那刻夏：**看起来，你现在很轻松嘛。

**"Zandar":** Indeed. The experiment's variable will be eliminated by my enemy's very hands. A beautiful way to kill two birds with one stone, isn't it?

> **「赞达尔」：**当然。实验的变量会由我的敌手铲除，这难道不是一桩两全其美的妙事么？

**"Zandar":** You know very well that everything I said about that Remembrance pathstrider was true. Both my hypothesis regarding her identity, and my recommendations to Lord Dan Heng...

> **「赞达尔」：**阁下应当最为清楚，关于那位「记忆」行者，我袒露的情报句句属实。无论是对她身份的猜测，还是提供给丹恒阁下的建议……

**Anaxa:** Yes, I know. You haven't told a single lie so far.

> **那刻夏：**这话不假。到目前为止，你确实没有说谎。

**Anaxa:** But I noticed your thoughts would generate a curious ripple whenever you mentioned her. It's almost like...

> **那刻夏：**只不过我注意到，每当提及她时，你的思想总会泛起一阵涟漪。就好像……

**Anaxa:** You were deliberately trying to hide something into the depths of your brain.

> **那刻夏：**在刻意将什么东西藏进大脑深处。

**"Zandar":** Since you're talking in such a blunt way, I'm assuming you haven't actually found any concrete evidence.

> **「赞达尔」：**阁下如此挑明，想必是没找到什么证据吧？

**Anaxa:** That is correct. Thanks to that mechanical body, you can control the boundaries of your thoughts, opting to only show partial truths.

> **那刻夏：**不错。借由这具机械躯体，你能够控制思维的边界，只将部分真相拱手示人。

**Anaxa:** Pity that I still managed to grasp a shiver of fear that escaped your control, and I traced it upstream to discover one single word...

> **那刻夏：**但很可惜。我还是抓住了你没能抑制的一缕恐惧，顺藤摸瓜，翻出了三个字。

**"Zandar":** ...

> **「赞达尔」：**……

**Anaxa:** Ahahaha! Lycurgus, tell me—

> **那刻夏：**哈哈哈，吕枯耳戈斯，告诉我——

**Anaxa:** What is... the Demiurge?

> **那刻夏：**德谬歌，是为何物？

## 154　Earth, Bear the Suffering of All　大地啊，承载万物的苦难

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Radiant Scarwood" Grove of Epiphany　|　角色：Dan Heng; Geocles; Dan Feng; Tribbie; Trianne; Trinnon; "Spirithief" Bartholos (NPC); Hysilens; Anaxa; Aglaea; Castorice; Mydei; Hyacine; Cipher; Phainon; Taoran; Bailu; Blade; Jingliu; Jing Yuan; Labienus; Seneca; Verginia; Apollonius; Cerydra; Terravox; Trailblazer; Baiheng; March 7th; Himeko; Welt; Dan Heng • Permansor Terrae; Cerces; Seele; Bronya; Dan Heng • Imbibitor Lunae; Phantylia; Dominicus　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Blazing Sun, Illuminate the Path for the Lost'' completed

### Head to "Radiant Scarwood" Grove of Epiphany

> *The Blazing Sun ravaged the altar beneath the Grove of Epiphany, which sealed the "Earth." By the time I arrived, the Remembrance already warped the Grove. Destruction is accidental, yet inevitable. I must press on, deeper into the mist.*

**Dan Heng:** The Grove...

> **丹恒：**树庭……

**Dan Heng:** Even the branches and leaves of the sacred tree have been dyed gold. Phainon gave his all for (Trailblazer).

> **丹恒：**连圣树的枝叶都被染成了金色，为了开拓者，白厄倾注了全力。

**Dan Heng:** Now that the Destruction has quelled the memoria tide, it's time for me to go deeper into the Grove.

> **丹恒：**既然「毁灭」已将忆潮彻底驱散，该深入其中了。

**Flustered Chimera:** ...Awoo!

> **慌张的奇美拉：**…嗷！

**Dan Heng:** Who's there!?

> **丹恒：**谁在那？！

### Talk with the mysterious Mountain Dweller

*(Approach the Mountain Dweller)*

**???:** That fiery blaze engulfed the Grove and woke all creatures from their slumber...

> **？？？：**那道烈焰，烧毁了树庭，惊扰了众生的沉眠……

**???:** And you, visitor from beyond the sky... You have no intention of listening to the cries of the "Earth."

> **？？？：**而你，天外来客…你甚至无意聆听「大地」的悲鸣。

**Dan Heng:** You are...?

> **丹恒：**你是……

**???:** Are you surprised? Demigods with golden blood are not solely limited to Kephale's children. Is it not natural that the offspring of Georios would take on their duty?

> **？？？：**很意外么？金血、半神，并不为刻法勒之子独有。吉奥利亚的子嗣，自当成为它的脊梁。

**Terravox:** I am Terravox, avatar of the earth, divine protector of the myriad living beings.

> **荒笛：**我乃荒笛，大地之化身，万千生灵的守护神。

**Dan Heng:** What a coincidence. I was just told by someone to watch out for you.

> **丹恒：**真是无巧不成书，刚有人提醒我要注意你。

**Dan Heng:** Answer me: Why would the Dromas King of the legends, the demigod who should have fallen, appear in a humanoid form?

> **丹恒：**回答我：神话中的地兽之王，理应陨落的半神，为何会以人形现身？

**Terravox:** You already know the answer: Is the Transmutation Arcanum not precisely your specialty, descendant of the Permanence?

> **荒笛：**明知故问：那化龙妙法不正是你的看家本领么，「不朽」的后裔？

**Dan Heng:** How do you know about...

> **丹恒：**你怎么会知道……

**Dan Heng:** Was it "March 7th"?

> **丹恒：**是「三月七」？

**Terravox:** Exactly. Thanks to her, I was able to escape into the memoria tide and disappear from the world's memories.

> **荒笛：**没错。拜她所赐，我才能遁入忆潮，从世人的记忆中消失。

**Dan Heng:** So you didn't die. Your "fall" was just a lie. You cast aside your duty as a Flame-Chaser and joined her. Why?

> **丹恒：**你没有死，所谓「陨落」只是一场骗局。不惜背离逐火的使命，也要和她搭上关系，为什么？

**Terravox:** To survive. For a dromas, life is no more than "existence"...

> **荒笛：**为了生存。对地兽而言，生命不过「存续」二字……

**Terravox:** The birds and beasts of this world have struggled to survive since time immemorial. Yet, unlike humans, they are unable to live on in the Worldbearer's memories.

> **荒笛：**飞禽、走兽、跨越亘古，艰难求生。却无法像人类一样，在「负世」的记忆中长存。

**Terravox:** But Evernight is the master of Remembrance, and I saw your story in the Veil of Evernight—

> **荒笛：**但长夜月，她是「记忆」的主宰。在「永夜之帷」中，我看见你的故事——

**Ichor Memosprite:**█ ██ █Vidyadhara █ ██ the Permanence █ dragon transmutation ██ █ █resurrec █tion█ ████ █

> **金血忆灵：**█ ██ █持明 █ ██ 不朽 █ 化龙 ██ █ █起死回 █生█ ████ █

**Dan Heng:** The path you chose to survive will lead to your death, demigod.

> **丹恒：**死地求生，你选错路了，半神。

**Terravox:** And what of it? This body is soaked in filthy golden blood...

> **荒笛：**那又如何？污浊的金血，浸入此身……

**Terravox:** The "Earth" is hungry, and only the Permanence can satisfy it!

> **荒笛：**「大地」饿了，只有「不朽」能填满它的胃袋！

*(Enter battle)*

(Wave 1: Ichor Memosprite: Pheasant ×2, Ichor Memosprite: Canine ×2)

**Dan Heng:** This madness seems exactly like a mara-struck.

> **丹恒：**这疯狂的气息，简直与魔阴身无异。

(Wave 2: Cloud Knights Patroller ×3)

**Dan Heng:** Cloud Knights? How is that possible...?

> **丹恒：**云骑？怎么可能……

(Wave 3: Disciples of Sanctus Medicus: Shape Shifter ×2, Cloud Knights Patroller ×2)

**Dan Heng:** It's endless... Has the memoria tide returned?

> **丹恒：**无穷无尽…忆潮卷土重来了么？

**???:** The fires of abomination reignite...

> **？？？：**孽火既生……

**???:** Why not join that Water-Mirrored Moon and be fully imbibed?

> **？？？：**何不伴那水中月，一饮而尽？

*(After the battle)*

**Dan Heng:** Is this some... disordered memory?

> **丹恒：**这是…错乱的记忆？

**Dan Heng:** ...Ugh!

> **丹恒：**——唔！

**???:** The mists of the past shall drift away.

> **？？？：**过往云烟，理应飘散。

**Dan Heng:** What...

> **丹恒：**什么……

**???:** Scatter, fade, turn to foam.

> **？？？：**散去，消逝，化作浮沫。

**???:** Do you not remember me?

> **？？？：**不记得我了么？

**???:** After all, I am the heavy burden you must bear...

> **？？？：**罢了，我毕竟是你要背负的重责……

**???:** How could I be so easily forsaken?

> **？？？：**如何能被轻易遗落？

**Dan Heng:** Dan... Feng?

> **丹恒：**丹…枫？

**Dan Heng:** Is this another Memosnatcher trick?

> **丹恒：**又是窃忆者的把戏？

**"Dan Feng":** Vidyadhara are supposed to forget the past during hatching rebirth, but the Preceptors interfered back then and allowed "me" to remain deep in your consciousness.

> **「丹枫」：**持明蜕生本该遗忘前尘旧事，但龙师们当年从中作梗，让「我」残留在你心识深处……

**"Dan Feng":** If you do not wish to use that bloodstained name, you may call me "Obliviated Memory."

> **「丹枫」：**若不愿提起那染血的旧名，唤我「忘却的记忆」也好。

**Dan Heng:** ...That's not necessary. Though we've long parted ways, I won't ever forget you.

> **丹恒：**…不必了。纵使早已分道扬镳，我也不会忘记你。

**"Dan Feng":** So, who is that giant?

> **「丹枫」：**所以，那巨人是谁？

**Dan Heng:** That is Amphoreus's "Earth" demigod. He should be on our side, but he was led astray and intends to seize the power of dragon transmutation.

> **丹恒：**翁法罗斯「大地」的半神。他本该站在我们这边，却受人蛊惑，意图夺取化龙之力。

**"Dan Feng":** The dragon descends upon the earth, and all beings look to it in reverence. You and I know that all life in the universe covets Permanence.

> **「丹枫」：**龙临大地，万类仰止。寰宇生灵皆贪图「不朽」，你我再清楚不过。

**Dan Heng:** Are you trying to say I'll end up like you?

> **丹恒：**你想说，我会重蹈你的覆辙？

**"Dan Feng":** One day you will, and as I see it, that day won't be far from the end of this journey.

> **「丹枫」：**总有一天，你会的。依我见，此行终点离那时分相当近了。

**Dan Heng:** Let's move on to another topic. I have no intention of passing judgment on "Dan Feng," and I have even less desire to become entangled with him again.

> **丹恒：**结束这个话题吧。我不打算评判「丹枫」，更无意再与他产生纠葛。

**Dan Heng:** I only have one concern right now: bringing my companions back safely.

> **丹恒：**现在，我只关心一件事，那就是让伙伴们平安归来。

**Dan Heng:** You can either help me deal with these abominations or return to the shadows of memory, Dan Feng.

> **丹恒：**要么帮我抵御孽物，要么就退回记忆的阴影里去，丹枫。

**"Dan Feng":** Alright. The filthy remnants of the memoria tide still run rampant. Let me accompany you through these ruins. You could use the help.

> **「丹枫」：**也罢。忆潮残秽仍在肆虐，有我陪同，你在这废墟中也能多一道助力。

**"Dan Feng":** And presumably, that giant who craves the power of dragons won't just let things rest.

> **「丹枫」：**那渴求龙之力的巨人，想必也不会善罢甘休。

**Dan Heng:** Do as you please. I'm moving ahead.

> **丹恒：**随你。我要动身了。

### Venture into the Grove, seek out Terravox

> *The Blazing Sun ravaged the altar beneath the Grove of Epiphany, which was the seal of "Earth." By the time I arrived, the Grove was already warped by the Remembrance. Destruction is accidental, yet inevitable. I must press on, deeper into the mist. Dan Feng is within the "memories" of this vessel of mine? If you'd lend your strength, then stay your path with me...*

*(After previous dialogue)*

**Dan Heng:** Speaking of Terravox, his aggression just now was probably some distraction.

> **丹恒：**说回荒笛。方才的挑衅，恐怕只是佯攻。

**"Dan Feng":** His madness is plain as day.

> **「丹枫」：**其人疯狂，显而易见。

**Dan Heng:** I agree.

> **丹恒：**所见略同。

*(Approach Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon)*

**"Trianne":** Phew, it's all good now. Janus's blessing is full, and the Hidden Passage can be used again!

> **「缇安」：**嘿咻，这下就好了，雅努斯祝福满满，密径可以重新使用啦！

**"Trinnon":** Now, the Imperator's legions can launch a surprise attack from the Grove.

> **「缇宁」：**这样一来，凯撒陛下的军团就可以从树庭发动奇袭了。

**"Tribbie":** Who knows if Terravox will be able to succeed...

> **「缇宝」：**不知道荒笛那边能不能成功呀……

**"Tribbie":** Huh? Who is this warrior?

> **「缇宝」：**咦，这位战士，你是……

**Dan Heng:** Lady Tribios, I am Dan Heng, (Trailblazer)'s companion.

> **丹恒：**缇里西庇俄丝女士。是我，开拓者的同伴，丹恒。

**"Trinnon":** Dan Heng? Nice to meet you. I didn't realize (Trailblazer) had more companions in addition to little Cyrene.

> **「缇宁」：**丹恒？初次见面。开拓者的伙伴，原来不止小昔涟吗？

**"Dan Feng":** There is no need to elaborate further. They are merely a collection of your memories, just like me.

> **「丹枫」：**我看，不必多费口舌了。她们和我一样，不过是你的一簇记忆。

**Dan Heng:** No. If they were my memories, they would recognize me.

> **丹恒：**不对。倘若是我的记忆，她们理应认得我才是。

**"Trianne":** Dan... Dan Heng?

> **「缇安」：**丹…丹恒？

**"Trianne":** Even though we don't know you, we'll still ask you to get somewhere safe. A terrible battle is about to break out here.

> **「缇安」：**虽然*我们*不认识你，但这里马上要发生一场可怕的战斗，还是快去安全的地方吧！

**"Trinnon":** Hysilens... I mean, "Dux Gladiorum" is not far ahead. The evacuation efforts are almost over, so go now, or it might be too late.

> **「缇宁」：**海瑟音…唔，「剑旗爵」就在前面不远，疏散工作快要完成了。快去吧，不然可能会赶不上。

**Dan Heng:** Hysilens...

> **丹恒：**海瑟音……

**Dan Heng:** I see. In that case, I'll go look for her now. Thank you.

> **丹恒：**明白了，我这就去找她。谢谢你们。

**"Tribbie":** No problem! We need to go deliver some messages, so we'll be off. Please take care, Dan Heng!

> **「缇宝」：**不客气！*我们*还得去传递讯息，先走了——你要保重喔，丹恒！

**"Dan Feng":** Memory's crazed ramblings. What do you think?

> **「丹枫」：**记忆的诳语。你怎么看？

**Dan Heng:** It's too early to reach a conclusion, but this memory is most definitely not mine.

> **丹恒：**暂时不成结论，但这道回忆显然不属于我。

**"Dan Feng":** There are two possibilities: This memory either belongs to Terravox, or it belongs to (Trailblazer), the person you're desperately looking for.

> **「丹枫」：**两种可能性：它属于「荒笛」，或是你苦苦追寻的开拓者。

**"Dan Feng":** But the probability of the latter is very low.

> **「丹枫」：**但后者的概率，微乎其微。

**"Dan Feng":** You should know better than I do. After that final battle, even the First Genius couldn't determine where he/she went.

> **「丹枫」：**你应该比我清楚，那场决战过后，就连第一位天才都无法断定他/她何去何从。

**Dan Heng:** Correct, but the conversation I just had gave me an idea.

> **丹恒：**不错。但刚才的对话给了我启发。

**Dan Heng:** Let's forget "Evernight" for now. In the Vortex of Genesis, there was someone else who went missing along with (Trailblazer).

> **丹恒：**且不论「长夜月」。在创世涡心，还有个人和开拓者一道失去了音讯。

**Dan Heng:** I still have a chance to make contact with her.

> **丹恒：**我还有机会和她取得联络。

**Dan Heng:** And the way I'll do it... is right here in the Grove.

> **丹恒：**方法…就在树庭中。

*(After passing through Janus's Hidden Passage)*

**Desperate Soldier:** N-No! Stay back, you cursed beast...

> **绝望的战士：**不、不！别过来，你这头该死的野兽……

*(After entering the area of Butterfly Husk Embers)*

**Dan Heng:** There's not a single sign of movement here...

> **丹恒：**空无一物，一片死寂……

**Dan Heng:** That was probably just the echoes of the memoria tide.

> **丹恒：**大约只是忆潮的回声吧。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Bartholos:** Hehehe! Look who it is! It's the water-splashing poser! Didn't think I'd catch you here!

> **巴特鲁斯：**桀桀桀！玩水的装酷小子，没想到会在这里遇见你！

**Dan Heng:** Are you... the Spirithief? Bartholos?

> **丹恒：**你是…贼灵？巴特鲁斯？

**Bartholos:** You can't get past this.

> **巴特鲁斯：**这下面你们过不去的。

**"Dan Feng":** I see. Thank you for the advice. My cloudhymn magic will take it from here.

> **「丹枫」：**原来如此，多谢提醒，交给我的云吟术吧。

**Bartholos:** Save your energy for more important stuff! This is a piece of cake. Leave it to yours truly!

> **巴特鲁斯：**把你们的体力留在更重要的事情上吧，这个简单，交给本大爷我吧。

- ▸ What happened to your voice?

> ▸ 你的声音怎么了？

**Bartholos:** It's all because of that crazy lady with pink hair and red eyes! In order to stop her from monitoring me, I sealed my voice, hehehe!

> **巴特鲁斯：**都怪那个粉毛红眼睛的疯女人！为了不让她监视本大爷的一举一动，我特地把自己的嗓门封印起来了，桀桀桀！

- ▸ Then I'll leave it to you.

> ▸ 那就交给你了。

**Bartholos:** There are plenty of ways to get through here. Hehehe, it's time for me to show you what I can do!

> **巴特鲁斯：**要想通过这里，方法多的是——桀桀桀，该轮到本大爷大显神通了！

### Help Spirithief pass through this area

> *The Blazing Sun ravaged the altar beneath the Grove of Epiphany, which was the seal of "Earth." By the time I arrived, the Grove was already warped by the Remembrance. Destruction is accidental, yet inevitable. I must press on, deeper into the mist. The spirit water has stagnated in the Grove of Epiphany. Resolve this with the Spirithief's tricks.*

*(After engaging with the Spirithief portal)*

*(Displays Bartholos's POV 1 Tutorial)*

**Bartholos:** Hehehe, it's time for me to show what I can do!

> **巴特鲁斯：**桀桀桀，轮到本大爷大显神通了！

### Help Spirithief block the water outlet

*(When interacting with the water outlet)*

**Bartholos:** That's the water outlet. If we could block it... the water in this room would drain naturally.

> **巴特鲁斯：**那地方是出水口，要是能堵上的话…这房间里的水会自然排空。

*(After fixing the spirit water leak)*

**Bartholos:** Hehehe, I've blocked all the inlets here! I'm truly the "lord of obstacles"!

> **巴特鲁斯：**桀桀桀，这里的所有出口都被我堵上了！本大爷不愧是「添堵大王」！

**Bartholos:** Wait... I still need to make a ladder for them... let's use this Prophecy Tablet.

> **巴特鲁斯：**慢着…还得帮他们搭个梯子…就用这个预言算碑吧？

### Help Spirithief modify the Prophecy Tablet

*(After investigating the Prophecy Tablet)*

**Bartholos:** The water problem is now all gone! Go take the elevator over there if you want to keep going.

> **巴特鲁斯：**水位的问题已经完美解决了！要继续前进，就去搭那边的电梯吧！

### Venture into the Grove, seek out Terravox

> *The Blazing Sun ravaged the altar beneath the Grove of Epiphany, which was the seal of "Earth." By the time I arrived, the Grove was already warped by the Remembrance. Destruction is accidental, yet inevitable. I must press on, deeper into the mist. The chaotic memories here keep growing. Besides mine, whose else could they be?*

**Dan Heng:** We're here. Now it's time to awaken the person we're looking for.

> **丹恒：**就是这里，是时候唤醒我们要找的人了。

**"Dan Feng":** A pool of spirit water...

> **「丹枫」：**一汪灵水……

**"Dan Feng":** I see. You plan to resonate with the waters of this world.

> **「丹枫」：**原来如此，你准备和这个世界的水域共鸣。

**Dan Heng:** Compared to the currents of my home, the waters of Amphoreus are... rather untamed. Subduing the waters wasn't easy, but I've gotten the hang of it now.

> **丹恒：**和我故乡的洋流相比，翁法罗斯的水体要更…不羁。驯服它的过程有些艰难，还好我已大概摸清了其中的门路。

**Dan Heng:** "Under the raucous steps of their frenzied dance, the roar of celebration..."

> **丹恒：**「于它狂欢的舞步之下，以盛会的喧嚣……」

**Dan Heng:** "...Calls forth the spirit water's memories."

> **丹恒：**「…唤起灵水的记忆。」

**???:** How incredible...

> **？？？：**不可思议……

**"Hysilens":** There are still survivors in Amphoreus, and this one even knows how to use the arts of water.

> **「海瑟音」：**翁法罗斯竟还有生者——此人还懂得唤水之术。

**Dan Heng:** Very good...

> **丹恒：**太好了……

**Dan Heng:** It's nice to meet the "Ocean" demigod, Lady Hysilens.

> **丹恒：**很高兴见到您，「海洋」的半神，海瑟音女士。

**"Hysilens":** A green dragonfish... Are you Dan Heng? I hope I've remembered your name correctly.

> **「海瑟音」：**青色的龙鱼…是丹恒？我应该没有记错你的名字。

**"Hysilens":** Scalegorge Waterscape is your home, isn't it? In (Trailblazer)'s memories, I saw you two standing side-by-side.

> **「海瑟音」：**鳞渊境是你的家乡，对么？在开拓者的记忆中，我看见你们并肩而立。

**Dan Heng:** That's fortunate. It means I won't have to introduce myself. I can get straight to the point.

> **丹恒：**万幸，这样也能免去自我介绍，直接表明来意了。

**Dan Heng:** Tell me, where are "Evernight" and (Trailblazer)?

> **丹恒：**请告诉我，「长夜月」和开拓者如今身在何方？

**"Hysilens":** The stranger snatched away the "Deliverer" right before my eyes. I commanded the waters to surge after them, but layered cliffs blocked the water's current...

> **「海瑟音」：**那位陌生人在我眼前掠走了「救世主」。我号令众水奔涌追逐，却被层岩阻断了流向……

**"Hysilens":** You must be careful. "Earth" has abandoned the Flame-Chase mission. Though Terravox still lives, they are not an ally of the Trailblaze.

> **「海瑟音」：**务必小心，「大地」背离了逐火的使命。荒笛依然在世，它绝非「开拓」的盟友。

**Dan Heng:** That's no surprise. I've already exchanged blows with that mad demigod.

> **丹恒：**不意外。那坠入疯狂的半神已和我交过手了。

**"Hysilens":** I didn't think... the worst would still come to pass.

> **「海瑟音」：**没想到…最坏的状况还是发生了。

**Dan Heng:** He also intends to get his hands on a dangerous power. As his comrade-in-arms, do you know what his goal is?

> **丹恒：**不仅如此，他还意图染指危险的力量。身为战友，或许你知道他的目的是什么？

**"Hysilens":** Terravox has only ever cared for life born from this soil. If they covet powers from beyond the sky, I suppose it's out of the same care.

> **「海瑟音」：**荒笛眼里，从来只有自土地中诞生的生命。贪图天外的不可名状之力，我想动机也不外如是。

**"Hysilens":** Even so, their behavior is... far too unusual.

> **「海瑟音」：**可即便如此，它的举止…也太异常了。

**"Dan Feng":** I presume they wouldn't have made such a drastic and irreversible decision if they had not exhausted all other means.

> **「丹枫」：**想来，若非油尽灯枯，它也不必如此破釜沉舟。

**"Dan Feng":** But embracing the Permanence may result in even more misfortune.

> **「丹枫」：**但拥抱「不朽」，恐怕只会落得更不幸的下场。

**Dan Heng:** While I admire that demigod's willpower, my position has not changed.

> **丹恒：**这位半神的意志值得尊敬，但我的立场不会变。

**Dan Heng:** It seems that if I want to find "Evernight," I will have to confront Earth. Since my opponent has already turned into a vicious beast...

> **丹恒：**看来，要找到「长夜月」，与「大地」一战无可避免。既然对方已沦为害兽……

**Dan Heng:** What I do won't weigh on my conscience.

> **丹恒：**也正好免去我的心理负担。

**"Hysilens":** ...Then allow my specter to accompany you, Lord Dan Heng. As the demigod who witnessed the end of this world...

> **「海瑟音」：**…那么，丹恒阁下，请允许我的分身一同随行。作为见证此世末路的半神……

**"Hysilens":** At least let me sing an elegy for my last comrade.

> **「海瑟音」：**至少，让我亲自为最后一位战友送去挽歌。

**"Dan Feng":** That doesn't sound like something you would normally say.

> **「丹枫」：**不像你平日会说的话。

**Dan Heng:** Huh?

> **丹恒：**什么？

**"Dan Feng":** "What I do won't weigh on my conscience." I can certainly guess what you mean by that.

> **「丹枫」：**「正好免去我的心理负担」——言外之意，我能猜到一二。

**Dan Heng:** I will never hesitate for my companions.

> **丹恒：**为了同伴，我不会有分毫犹豫。

**"Dan Feng":** ...

> **「丹枫」：**……

**Dan Heng:** I am a Nameless, and what I'm going to do...

> **丹恒：**我是一名「无名客」，而我要做的事……

**Dan Heng:** Is to defend all the hopes that are on the verge of fading away.

> **丹恒：**就是捍卫一切行将飘逝的希望。

**"Dan Feng":** Then I misspoke.

> **「丹枫」：**是我失言了。

**"Dan Feng":** That is exactly how the "guard" of the Astral Express should act.

> **「丹枫」：**星穹列车的「护卫」…理应如此。

*(After previous dialogue)*

**Dan Heng:** If you please, Lady Hysilens.

> **丹恒：**那就劳驾了，海瑟音女士。

**"Hysilens":** Hold onto this current, for it still carries the warmth of Gray Fry.

**"Hysilens":** Hopefully, it will lead us to the depths where our companion is hidden.

*(Approach the memories of the people, idle quotes)*

**Sword-Wielding Soldier:** Lord Terravox, where are you?

**Cowering Citizen:** Lord Terravox has betrayed us...

*(When approaching the elevator)*

**Terravox:** Helektra... Is that you?

> **荒笛：**海列屈拉…是你？

**"Hysilens":** Who's that...?

> **「海瑟音」：**这声音是……

**Terravox:** You're right on time...

> **荒笛：**来得正好……

**Terravox:** Let the "Ocean" sink into the memoria tide...

> **荒笛：**就让「海洋」一同沉入忆潮……

**Terravox:** And become part of the sacrifice to resurrect the "Earth"!

> **荒笛：**加入复活「大地」的献祭！

*(While proceeding to the marked locations, memories of Chrysos Heirs and those from Dan Heng's past appear with their words floating alongside them. When approached, the memories of the Chrysos Heirs become non-hostile Ichor Memosprites and the memories of the High-Cloud Quintet become hostile Ichor Memosprites.)*

*(While walking through the first room)*

**"Aglaea" (?):**█ ███Join█us█ ██ ██

> **「阿格莱雅」（？）：**█ ███加入█我们█  ██ ██

**"Tribbie" (?):**█ █You are me██are█us█ ██ ██

> **「缇宝」（？）：**█ █你就是我██就是█我们█  ██ ██

**"Anaxa" (?):**███From█ █permanence██gain█eternal life█ █

> **「那刻夏」（？）：**███于不█ █朽中██得█永生█ █

*(While exiting the first room)*

**"Mydei" (?):**█Throw yourself into █the █forge████ █ █

> **「万敌」（？）：**█投身 █熔 █炉████ █ █

**"Castorice" (?):**█Merge██ █as█one███ █ █

> **「遐蝶」（？）：**█融合██ █为█一███ █ █

**"Hyacine" (?):**███Keep going█keep█going██ █ █going█ █

> **「风堇」（？）：**███存续█存█续██ █ █续█ █

*(While walking along the tree branch)*

**"Cipher" (?):**███Do not█run from██ █ your█fate█ █

> **「赛飞儿」（？）：**███不必█逃避██ █ 命█运█ █

**"Phainon" (?):**██Use the Permanence█to fill██the█"Earth" ███ ██

> **「白厄」（？）：**██用「不朽」█饱腹██「大█地」 ███ ██

*(While approaching the elevetor)*

**"Taoran" (?):** Vidyadhara might not even be considered people if we did not resort to beastly actions!

> **「涛然」（？）：**若不做兽行，持明更遑论为人！

**"Bailu" (?):** Why did Dan Feng choose me all those years ago?

> **「白露」（？）：**为什么，丹枫当年选择了我？

**Terravox:** Long's Scion... fall into oblivion!

> **荒笛：**龙裔，沉沦吧…！

**"Hysilens":** The memoria tide surges violently. Hold on, and don't let your focus be swept away.

> **「海瑟音」：**忆潮汹涌而来。坚持住，别被掠走心神。

*(After descending the elevator, the ground begins to shake)*

**Dan Heng:** The whole Grove is shaking...

> **丹恒：**整个树庭，都在震颤……

**"Dan Feng":** How are you doing? Can you keep going?

> **「丹枫」：**如何，坚持得住么？

**Dan Heng:** Of course.

> **丹恒：**当然。

*(While walking through the hallway)*

**"Jingliu" (?):** Of five people...

> **「镜流」（？）：**人有五名……

**"Jing Yuan" (?):** Three must pay a price...

> **「景元」（？）：**代价有三……

*(When approaching the final memories)*

**"Blade" (?):** You... are one of them.

> **「刃」（？）：**你，是其中之一。

*(While walking through the end of the hall)*

**Dan Heng:** Is it over...?

> **丹恒：**结束了么……

**"Dan Feng":** It seems so. These phantoms that are haunting you...

> **「丹枫」：**看来是了。那些侵扰你的幻影……

**"Dan Feng":** Are indeed here to bring you to your demise.

> **「丹枫」：**诚是为置你于死地而来啊。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Dan Heng:** It's unusually quiet here compared to before.

> **丹恒：**相比刚才，这里静得有些出奇了。

**"Hysilens":** Don't let your guard down. The memoria tide has become even more violent...

> **「海瑟音」：**别大意，忆潮变得更汹涌了……

**"Dan Feng":** Is that...

> **「丹枫」：**那是……

### Venture into the Grove, review the disordered memory

> *The Blazing Sun ravaged the altar beneath the Grove of Epiphany, which was the seal of "Earth." By the time I arrived, the Grove was already warped by the Remembrance. Destruction is accidental, yet inevitable. I must press on, deeper into the mist. The chaotic memories here keep growing. Besides mine... Dan Feng's memories are here too?*

*(While descending the walkway)*

**???:** This is an interesting-looking wine jar. Was it Baiheng who brought it?

> **？？？：**这壶酒…模样倒是新奇。又是白珩带来的？

**???:** It's a specialty from Thalassa that she brought and left here just for this gathering.

> **？？？：**是她留下的塔拉萨特产，专为这一趟聚会准备的。

**???:** But she herself didn't come.

> **？？？：**结果她自己倒是没来。

*(Approach the memories)*

**"Dan Feng":** ...Your mind retains far more of the past than I had imagined.

> **「丹枫」：**…你脑海中遗留的过往，远比我想的更多。

**Dan Heng:** I've seen this farewell more than once in my dreams.

> **丹恒：**这场战前告别，我不止一次在梦中见过。

**"Dan Feng":** The previous life is just an immersia to a newly-born Vidyadhara. You know rationally that these are "your" memories, but it doesn't feel like you experienced them yourself.

> **「丹枫」：**对于新生的持明，前世只是一场幻戏。你明白这是「你」的记忆，但无法感同身受。

**"Dan Feng":** I used dragon transmutation for selfish reasons, and close friends turned to enemies.

> **「丹枫」：**为一己私欲，擅用化龙之力，让昔日亲友化作仇敌……

**"Dan Feng":** You probably want to ask me if it was worth it.

> **「丹枫」：**你一定会问我：如此代价，是否值得？

**Jing Yuan:** The Military Affairs Office is placing more importance on the Yuque's battle this time, and the Yaoqing called her back for a patrol mission.

> **景元：**军务厅对此次「玉阙」之战颇为重视，「曜青」急急召她回去执行斥候任务。

**Yingxing:** Some people are born with adventure in their blood and can't stop for even a moment. No matter, I'll drink her portion for her!

> **应星：**有些人就是血里有风，停不下一时半刻。无妨，她那份我来喝就好！

**Jing Yuan:** We're already toasting? Why don't we wait for Dan Feng?

> **景元：**怎么就要举杯了？不等等丹枫吗？

**Yingxing:** As always, the high elder has endless meetings with the Preceptors that he can't get away from. Let's not wait on him.

> **应星：**龙尊大人，自然是有无休无止的龙师会议要开，一时半刻散不了场。不等他了。

**Yingxing:** Pah... Isn't this just water!?

> **应星：**呸…这不是水吗？！

**Jingliu:** I'll relay her exact words: It is a mistake to drink too much before a battle, so I'll leave a jar of spring water from Thalassa's crystal palace.

> **镜流：**我转述她的原话：战事当前，贪杯误事，我就先在这儿放一瓮塔拉萨水晶宫的涌泉。

**Jingliu:** She said that wine only has flavor after a triumphant return.

> **镜流：**她说，酒要在凯旋后喝才有滋味。

**Yingxing:** ...Why didn't you say so earlier?

> **应星：**…你不早说。

**Jingliu:** I didn't want to go along with her, but she insisted on seeing your expression after your first sip.

> **镜流：**我本来不想的，奈何她非要看看你喝下第一口的表情。

**Jing Yuan:** Any gift from a friend, whether it is water or wine, is rich in flavor all the same.

> **景元：**水也好、酒也罢，若是朋友所赠，便是同等醇厚。

**"Dan Feng":** ...

**"Dan Feng":** I see you still speak as if every sentence must add something of profound value.

> **丹枫：**你每句话都要上价值的旧疾还没痊愈吗？

**Dan Feng:** I see you still speak as if every sentence must add something of profound value.

**Jing Yuan:** You're just in time! The punishment for making us all wait should be three cups of wine.

> **景元：**来得正好，大家都在等你呢。要不你自罚三杯吧！

**Dan Feng:** Spare me, please. I've already suffered enough next to the ancient sea.

> **丹枫：**饶了我吧，我在古海边喝的苦水还少吗？

**Dan Feng:** I've convinced the Preceptors: Vidyadhara cloudhymn magi will join the battle on the frontlines, and our armies will fight side-by-side.

> **丹枫：**我已说服龙师们，这一战将有持明云吟士亲赴前线，与我军并肩作战。

**Yingxing:** It seems like you can now carry out your plan for dealing with the "Ketu Mirage," Jing Yuan.

> **应星：**看来，你那对付「计都蜃楼」的计划可以实行了，景元。

**Jing Yuan:** Thank you for standing your ground against the dissenters, Dan Feng.

> **景元：**谢谢你力排众议，丹枫。

**Yingxing:** I never thought those Preceptors would ever relent. After all of this, countless Vidyadhara...

> **应星：**没想到那些龙师真会松口。此役之后，定然……

**Dan Feng:** Countless Vidyadhara will lose their lives, with no chance at a molting rebirth. We all understand this.

> **丹枫：**定然会有无数持明族有去无回，再无机会蜕鳞重生。这一点每个人都清楚。

**Dan Feng:** But the Vidyadhara cannot become a part of the Alliance's destiny if we do not share in its joys and sorrows. We would only be bystanders to everyone else's suffering.

> **丹枫：**但是，若不同甘共苦，持明便不能成为联盟命运的一员，而只是他人苦难的旁观者。

**Jingliu:** That is something we all understand as well.

> **镜流：**这一点，我们每个人同样清楚。

**Jingliu:** That is also why we've gathered here. Come, raise your cups. This toast is not for bidding farewell to each other...

> **镜流：**这便是我们相聚在此的理由。来吧，举杯吧，诸位。这一杯不是为我们彼此饯行……

**Jingliu:** But for honoring the soldiers who will not return, for honoring our companions.

> **镜流：**而是敬那些不再归还的征人，敬我们的同伴。

**"Dan Feng":** ...

> **「丹枫」：**……

**Jing Yuan:** Apologies, my reckless plan must have put a lot of pressure on you.

> **景元：**抱歉，我那乱来的计划一定让你背了很大压力。

**"Dan Feng":** Nonsense.

> **「丹枫」：**别说这种话。

**"Dan Feng":** If our positions were flipped, you'd support me no matter how reckless my plan was, right?

> **「丹枫」：**如果立场转换，不管我的计划多么乱来，你也一定会支持我的。不是吗？

**Jing Yuan:** Of course. But let's try not to be too reckless.

> **景元：**当然。但还是别太乱来吧。

**Jingliu:** When you make a decision like this, people will always ask, "Was it worth it?"... And you will also forever question yourself.

> **镜流：**做出这样的决定，总有人会问你「值得吗？」…而你也会反复诘问自己。

**Jingliu:** Do you know how you will answer that question?

> **镜流：**面对这种问题，你知道该如何回答吗？

**"Dan Feng":** I won't answer it. I will prove it.

> **「丹枫」：**我不会回答。我会证明。

**Jingliu:** That's right. My sword is my answer.

> **镜流：**没错，我以兵锋作答。

**Yingxing:** Thank you, Dan Feng! Thank you, Jing Yuan! Thank you, Jingliu! And thank you, Baiheng, wherever you've run off to!

> **应星：**谢谢你，丹枫！谢谢你，景元！谢谢你，镜流！谢谢你，不知道跑到哪里去了的白珩！

**"Dan Feng":** Ridiculous, how did you get drunk from spring water?

> **「丹枫」：**你这酒鬼，喝些泉水也能喝醉吗？

**Yingxing:** Hmph, I'm just full of joy! It feels like I've just forged a divine weapon with masterful craftsmanship!

> **应星：**哼，我只是觉得痛快！好似打造出一件良工神兵般畅快！

**Yingxing:** "I'd rather leave this world in a blaze than live until the end of time..." That's what I always firmly believed in the past. But I never truly felt like I was alive until I met all of you.

> **应星：**「宁如飞萤赴火，不作樗木长春」…过去，我打心底里一直这么觉得。但，多亏遇见了你们，我从未像现在这样，切实地感到自己正在活着。

**Yingxing:** Now I wish more than anything that I could just live a bit longer. No, pretend I didn't say that!

> **应星：**我从没这么想要多活片刻——不成，这话可说不得！

**"Dan Feng":** ...

> **「丹枫」：**……

**Dan Heng:** It's over.

> **丹恒：**结束了。

**"Dan Feng":** And you know exactly what happened to these five afterward.

> **「丹枫」：**这五人后来的命运，你也一清二楚。

**"Dan Feng":** So, can you understand it now, after both (Trailblazer) and March 7th have left you?

> **「丹枫」：**所以，在开拓者和三月七也离你而去的当下，你能理解了么？

**"Dan Feng":** Losing a dear friend with whom you've shared so much can never be considered a victory. It's a failure that torments you for the rest of your life. I could never accept that kind of fate.

> **「丹枫」：**失去休戚与共的挚友，绝对称不上胜利，那只是一场折磨终生的失败。这种命运，我绝不接受。

**Dan Heng:** So you made a decision.

> **丹恒：**你做出了选择。

**"Dan Feng":** I was the one who used the Transmutation Arcanum. I was the one who swore to bring Baiheng back to life.

> **「丹枫」：**使用化龙妙法的，是我。发誓要将白珩带回世间的，是我。

**"Dan Feng":** And the one who brought everything past the point of no return... that was also me.

> **「丹枫」：**令一切走向无可挽回的，也是我。

**Dan Heng:** Do you have any regrets, Dan Feng?

> **丹恒：**丹枫，你心中可有一丝悔意？

**"Dan Feng":** ...

> **「丹枫」：**……

**"Dan Feng":** Even if I could reverse time, I would not choose differently.

> **「丹枫」：**就算光阴能够倒转，我也不会做出第二种选择。

**"Dan Feng":** And if it had been Jingliu, Jing Yuan... or even Yingxing who died in battle, I'd still choose to do the same thing.

> **「丹枫」：**倘若战死的是镜流、景元…乃至应星，我的答案亦是如此。

**"Dan Feng":** Because only I could make that choice and protect all of this. Now, for the sake of your companions, for the sake of those two who walk alongside you...

> **「丹枫」：**因为只有我能做出选择，能捍卫这一切。现在，为了同伴，为了与你同行的那两人……

**"Dan Feng":** How much are you willing to sacrifice, Dan Heng?

> **「丹枫」：**丹恒，你愿意做出多少牺牲？

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Dan Heng:** Everything.

> **丹恒：**一切。

**"Dan Feng":** Your answer does not surprise me.

> **「丹枫」：**这回答，毫不意外。

**Dan Heng:** They are more than just my companions. They are the path I took that led me to where I am now.

> **丹恒：**他们/她们不止是我的同伴，也是我来时所经的路，引我至此。

**Dan Heng:** So they will also be beacons for when I am lost, leading me to the future, until my next "reincarnation."

> **丹恒：**所以，他们/她们也会成为我迷失时的路标，通向未来，直到下一个「轮回」。

**"Dan Feng":** Then let me see how you'll carry out your convictions.

> **「丹枫」：**那就让我看看，你要如何贯彻这道信念吧。

### Venture into the disordered memory and seek out Terravox

> *The Blazing Sun ravaged the altar beneath the Grove of Epiphany, which was the seal of "Earth." By the time I arrived, the Grove was already warped by the Remembrance. Destruction is accidental, yet inevitable. I must press on, deeper into the mist. Hysilens revealed the truth that the mysterious Mountain Dweller was not the real Terravox.*

*(After previous dialogue)*

**Dan Heng:** Let's leave this place and go settle things with that mad demigod.

> **丹恒：**离开这里，去找那疯狂的半神…做个了断。

*(While walking towards the elevator)*

**"Dux Fragoris" Labienus:** Terravox has already defected! Legions, charge with me!

> **「断锋爵」拉比努斯：**荒笛已经倒戈！军团，随我冲锋！

**"Dux Brumalis" Seneca:** Hah, a beast will always be a beast.

> **「冬霖爵」塞涅卡：**呵，野兽终究是野兽。

*(While passing through the hall)*

**"Dux Carminum" Verginia:** This campaign has been far too cruel...

> **「吟风爵」维吉妮娅：**这场战役未免太过残忍…

**"Dux Helkolithist" Apollonius:** Let them be a necessary sacrifice for the Flame-Chase.

> **「曳石爵」阿波罗尼：**让它成为逐火必要的牺牲吧。

*(While walking along the balcony)*

**Cerydra:** Seismic Walking Dragon, history shall remember you as a titan-slayer!

> **刻律德菈：**掣地的伏龙啊，你将为弑主留名青史！

**"Dan Feng":** Memories intersect. Another betrayal, another catastrophe.

> **「丹枫」：**记忆交错。又一场背叛，一场浩劫。

**Dan Heng:** Regardless of whose memory this is, it seems that Terravox has a leading role in it.

> **丹恒：**看来，无论这回忆属于谁，荒笛都是其中的主角。

**"Dan Feng":** A beast that's been "rebelling" its whole life... No wonder they descended into madness.

> **「丹枫」：**一生都在「反叛」的野兽…不难理解，它为何如此疯狂了。

*(While passing by the viewpoint of the Chasmic Geocore)*

**Dan Heng:** I see the destination that the Destruction points to.

> **丹恒：**看见了，「毁灭」指向的终点。

**"Dan Feng":** Golden blood is gurgling out... It's almost like a heart that has burst open.

> **「丹枫」：**金血汨汨而出…仿佛破溃的心脏。

**"Hysilens":** Follow the current and calm its frenzied pulse.

> **「海瑟音」：**循着水流，平息它疯狂的鼓动吧。

### Talk with Terravox, the Earth Demigod

*(Approach Terravox)*

**"Dan Feng":** As expected, someone's been waiting for us.

> **「丹枫」：**不出所料，有人已恭候我们多时了。

**"Hysilens":** ...

> **「海瑟音」：**……

**"Hysilens":** It's you...?

> **「海瑟音」：**怎会是你……

**???:** They are here...

> **？？？：**他们到了……

**???:** A Scion of Permanence... and your old comrade-in-arms.

> **？？？：**「不朽」的龙裔，还有…你昔日的战友。

**???:** Terravox, my old friend... Indulge yourself.

> **？？？：**我的老友，荒笛…尽情享用吧。

**???:** Let this memoria tide swallow everything.

> **？？？：**让这片忆潮，吞噬一切。

**"Hysilens":** Be prepared for the worst, Lord Dan Heng. This Mountain Dweller must not be underestimated...

> **「开山者」吉奥刻勒斯：**我才能重返人间，守望我的挚友——那为逐火的阴谋白白牺牲的「大地」半神。

**"Hysilens":** Geocles the Mountainbreaker... Earth's Dromas Rider, it's been a while.

> **「开山者」吉奥刻勒斯：**龙裔，你是这场献祭中最重要的供物：再坚韧的意志也终将被忆潮吞没，届时，你，还有你承载的力量，便能为我等所有……

**Dan Heng:** "Mountainbreaker"? He's... not Terravox?

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** I remember you, little fish, and your despicable master.

**"Hysilens":** I didn't think you were still alive.

> **「开山者」吉奥刻勒斯：**用「不朽」唤起坠落的巨龙，令「大地」长出不灭的血肉！

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** Death is just a return to the Earth for Mountain Dwellers. Thanks to Evernight's gift...

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** I was able to come back to the mortal realm and keep watch over my dear friend, the "Earth" demigod who was sacrificed to the Flame-Chase plot in vain.

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** Long's Scion, you are the most important offering in this ritual sacrifice. No matter how strong your will is, it will eventually be swallowed by the memoria tide. When that time comes, you and the powers you bear will serve us...

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** We will use the Permanence to awaken the fallen Giant Dragon and give the "Earth" an everlasting body!

**Dan Heng:** I see. So you pretended to be Terravox...

> **丹恒：**原来如此，你假称自己是荒笛……

**Dan Heng:** In order to lead us here into a trap.

> **丹恒：**是为了让我们追击至此，步入陷阱。

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** If it weren't for the Imperator's tricks, the name "Terravox" would be shared between us anyway.

**"Hysilens":** They made the right choice for Amphoreus's tomorrow.

> **「开山者」吉奥刻勒斯：**老友啊，看吧……

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** But the tomorrow you speak of only ever had a place for "humans."

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** Watch, my old friend...

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** I will once again start a fire that burns away the chains that shackle us!

**"Hysilens":** Why don't you leave this to me, Lord Dan Heng? Use that Hidden Passage to reach the opposite shore. Perhaps (Trailblazer) is there, deeper inside...

> **「开山者」吉奥刻勒斯：**我将再度扬起山火，烧毁那束缚你我的枷锁！

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Dan Heng:** No, I will stay here.

> **丹恒：**不，让我留在这里。

**"Dan Feng":** ...

> **「丹枫」：**……

**Dan Heng:** "Mountainbreaker," hero of the Mountain Dwellers. If I do not stay here and triumph over him, he will die an ugly death in delusions of the Permanence. That should not be his destiny.

> **丹恒：**「开山者」，山之民的英雄。若我不在这里得胜，他必将在「不朽」的妄执中丑陋死去。这不该是他的结局。

**Dan Heng:** And for the vicious beast that insists on obstructing the Astral Express and threatening the fate of the world...

> **丹恒：**而对于执意阻拦星穹列车，威胁世界命运的害兽……

**Dan Heng:** We've stood on the precipice countless times along this journey, forced to make difficult choices in the face of disaster. But this time...

> **丹恒：**这一路来，我们曾无数次站在悬崖边，被危难胁迫，做出艰难的选择。唯独这次——

**Dan Heng:** It's my turn to put some pressure on this world.

> **丹恒：**该轮到我，向这个世界施压了。

*(Enter battle against )*

**"Dan Feng":** This feeling, this scene... Hah, all too similar.

> **「丹枫」：**此情此景…呵，何其相似。

*(After the battle)*

**"Hysilens":** It's time to stop struggling, Geocles. Go to your death with dignity.

**Dan Heng:** Before that, tell me, what kind of deal did you make with Evernight? How was the great Mountainbreaker reduced to this?

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** ...

**???:** *bereaved roar*

> **？？？：** *哀伤的轰鸣*

**"Dan Feng":** That's...

> **「丹枫」：**这是……

**Terravox:** Leave it to me. I'll recount the Earth's heaviest past...

> **荒笛：**交给我吧。阐述「大地」至沉的过去……

**Terravox:** ...And sever its deepest delusion.

> **荒笛：**…断绝它至深的妄念。

**"Hysilens":** Terravox... Is that you?

> **「海瑟音」：**荒笛…是你？

**"Hysilens":** Your body... has become so frail.

> **「海瑟音」：**你的身躯…竟变得如此瘦弱。

**Terravox:** Even mountain ranges grow old and eventually die.

> **荒笛：**群山也会老去，乃至死亡。

**"Dan Feng":** But I can hear the sound of blood still surging through your veins, fast and strong...

> **「丹枫」：**但我能听到，你的血脉中依旧回荡着澎湃的声响……

**Dan Heng:** I suppose you are the true Earth demigod.

> **丹恒：**想必，你就是真正的「大地」半神。

**Terravox:** I am near my end, and the demigod title means nothing to me now. The being who stands here now is just the former comrade of the "Mountainbreaker"...

> **荒笛：**我已垂暮，半神的名讳毫无意义。屹立于此的，只是「开山者」曾经的战友……

**Terravox:** And the traitor who abandoned him.

> **荒笛：**和遗弃他的背叛者。

**"Dan Feng":** Traitor?

> **「丹枫」：**背叛者？

**"Hysilens":** Terravox was once a member of Geocles' Dromas Legion. This force was famous for its skilled riders and their brave dromases.

> **「海瑟音」：**「荒笛」，这名字曾属于吉奥刻勒斯的龙骑兵团。这支部队以善战的骑手和他们骁勇的大地兽闻名。

**"Hysilens":** Terravox themself was the Dromas King, the Giant Dragon who swore their life to the "Mountainbreaker."

> **「海瑟音」：**而它，便是与「开山者」同生共死的巨龙，大地兽之王。

**"Hysilens":** In the battle against Georios, the Imperator could not surmount this mountain and thus had to break it from the inside. Terravox's betrayal was key to the Flame-Chase Army's victory.

> **「海瑟音」：**吉奥里亚一战，面对这座难以逾越的高山，凯撒只能从内部瓦解。荒笛的倒戈成了逐火军致胜的关键。

**Terravox:** The age of the gods was coming to an end. Living beings had adapted to the changes amidst the rifts of the earth, and now they had to learn to survive in the crevices of the epics.

> **荒笛：**神的时代已然落幕。生灵在地裂中适应了改变，也必须学会在史诗的夹缝求存。

**Terravox:** Someone had to watch over the "Earth." So I agreed to a deal. I would take the Titan's divine authority and sink into the cliffs, using my flesh and blood forged of stone to mend the land. But...

> **荒笛：**需要有人来守望「大地」。所以，我接受了交易。接过泰坦神权，我沉入岩渊，以石铸的血肉弥合大陆。但……

**Terravox:** There was one "betrayal" I could never forget.

> **荒笛：**唯有一桩「背叛」，我无法释怀。

**Terravox:** The "Mountainbreaker"... refused to believe that was my choice, even till his dying breath. Faced with having to surrender to the Imperator, he chose to die instead.

> **荒笛：**「开山者」…他至死都不愿相信那是我的选择。面对凯撒劝降，他以死明志。

**"Dan Feng":** So this is why you decided to conspire with "Evernight."

> **「丹枫」：**所以，这才是你和「长夜月」合谋的原因。

**Terravox:** "Time's" strange visitor appeared before me. The great power from beyond the sky called the Remembrance... It's incredible.

> **荒笛：**「岁月」的陌客降临在我面前。名为「记忆」的天外伟力…不可思议。

**Terravox:** Geocles was able to be reborn as a "memosprite." He mumbled about the times we fought side by side, just like how all the lives this body carries speak to me.

> **荒笛：**化作「忆灵」，吉奥刻勒斯得以重生。他口中喃喃着我们并肩作战的时光，一如这具身躯承载的所有生灵。

**Terravox:** "Evernight" did not ask for much in return. She only wished for me to use the divine authority of "Earth" to hide her tracks.

> **荒笛：**「长夜」开出的价码并不高昂，只要我施行「大地」神权，为她遮蔽行迹。

**"Hysilens":** Since you're being so candid, does this mean... you've given up resisting?

> **「海瑟音」：**愿意如此坦诚，你…放弃抵抗了么？

**Terravox:** Hah, resistance...

> **荒笛：**呵，抵抗……

**Terravox:** For a beast on the brink of death, what is the point of resistance?

> **荒笛：**一头将死的野兽，何谈抵抗？

**"Hysilens":** I see... A thousand years have passed, and your mind has long since been shattered.

> **「海瑟音」：**这样吗，千年过去了，你的心神…也早已支离破碎。

**Terravox:** This frail body bears the weight of all those I've betrayed, and now, my only wish is to rest with them...

> **荒笛：**这副垂老的身躯将自己背叛的一切尽数承载。现在，我只想在那不变的往日里……

**Terravox:** In the unchanging past.

> **荒笛：**和他们一同长眠。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Terravox:** Go, Long's Scion. That fiery blaze has already burned through "Earth's" protection, revealing the secrets underneath...

> **荒笛：**去吧，龙裔。那道烈焰已经洞穿「大地」守护的秘密……

**Terravox:** The deepest depths in Amphoreus. The great tomb where all the past is buried.

> **荒笛：**翁法罗斯的至深之地，埋葬一切过往的大墓。

**Terravox:** The one you seek, "Evernight," walked out of that place. Before her arrival, no one knew of its existence.

> **荒笛：**你寻找的「长夜月」，正是从其中走出。在她之前，无人知晓其存在。

**"Dan Feng":** In other words, that is the place where "Evernight" came into being...

> **「丹枫」：**换言之，那是「长夜月」现身的地点……

**"Hysilens":** And it is also where she is now.

> **「海瑟音」：**也是她如今的所在。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Dan Heng:** You sank willingly into the past, and now your "memories" have turned into this form... Was it worth it?

> **丹恒：**不惜沉沦在过去中，你的「记忆」都变成了这幅样貌…值得么？

**Terravox:** At least I still remember.

> **荒笛：**至少，我还记得。

**Terravox:** As long as I never forget, they will live on.

> **荒笛：**只要不曾忘记，他们就还活着。

**Dan Heng:** Primitive and animalistic... I understand these instincts.

> **丹恒：**原始、兽性…我理解这种本能。

**Dan Heng:** Have you ever regretted anything?

> **丹恒：**你，后悔过吗？

**Terravox:** The earth is not a lonely island in the middle of the ocean. It is the sum of all the living things that stand on it.

> **荒笛：**大地并非海洋中的孤岛，而是其上万物的总和。

**Terravox:** I have never regretted defending the creatures of this world.

> **荒笛：**为捍卫世间生灵，我从未后悔。

**Dan Heng:** Then, a final question...

> **丹恒：**那么，最后一问……

**Dan Heng:** Terravox... After all this, how much are you willing to sacrifice for the power of the Permanence?

> **丹恒：**荒笛…时至今日，为了「不朽」龙力，你愿意做出多少牺牲？

**Terravox:** ...

> **荒笛：**……

**Terravox:** Everything.

> **荒笛：**一切。

**Terravox:** My journey up to here has allowed me to fully understood my destiny: No matter how I struggle, the "Earth" will never be able to reach the stars.

> **荒笛：**行路至此，我已彻底理解了自己的命运：无论如何抗争，「大地」终究无法迈向群星。

**Terravox:** But my compatriots deserve to continue their struggle. At the end of my life, I want to make one last effort to give them a chance to move on to the future...

> **荒笛：**但，我的同胞，他们值得继续抗争。在生命的尽头，我想最后为他们争取一次行向未来的机会……

**Terravox:** Even if it is a future I am not able to witness.

> **荒笛：**哪怕，那未来我已无法见证。

**"Dan Feng":** ...

> **「丹枫」：**……

**Dan Heng:** I understand. Then...

> **丹恒：**我明白了。那么……

**Dan Heng:** Join me in rebelling against this unjust fate once more.

> **丹恒：**和我一同，向不公的命运再发起一次反叛吧。

**Dan Heng:** Even if the world collapses, the "Earth" must remain unshaken.

> **丹恒：**即便世界崩落，「大地」也必须伫立——

**Dan Heng:** Give me the Coreflame that burns within you, and allow this immortal body of the Permanence to bear the furnace of life for you.

> **丹恒：**将你体内燃烧的火种交予我，由这具「不朽」的身躯为你承载生命的熔炉。

**Terravox:** Long's Scion... Are you willing to bring them all into the new world?

> **荒笛：**龙裔…你愿意将它们带往新世界么？

**Dan Heng:** I won't give you empty promises. Allow me to answer your question from a different perspective.

> **丹恒：**空洞的承诺，恐怕不是你想要的答案。让我试着从另一个角度作出回答吧。

**Dan Heng:** The Permanence is the path that dragons trod, and "eternity" is life's deepest desire. If you entrust the earth's living beings to me, they will live in symbiosis with the instincts in my blood.

> **丹恒：**「不朽」是龙的道途，「永恒」是生命至深的渴望。你若将大地的生灵托付予我，它们定会与我血脉中的本能共生。

**Dan Heng:** With this body as an ark, I will deliver them to tomorrow for you. That tomorrow won't be the end of Amphoreus's west wind...

> **丹恒：**以此身为舟，我会代你将它们送往明天。那里不是翁法罗斯的西风尽头……

**Dan Heng:** But a vast and boundless sea of stars.

> **丹恒：**而是一片浩瀚无垠的星海。

**Terravox:** A sea of stars...

> **荒笛：**星海……

**Dan Heng:** It's a future where all beings can Trailblaze side by side, not a prison made from Remembrance.

> **丹恒：**那是由众生并肩「开拓」的未来，而非一座将「记忆」用作耗材的囚笼。

**Terravox:** In the Veil of Evernight's memories, you've always... been someone with reservations.

> **荒笛：**在「永夜之帷」的记忆中，你自始至终…都是个有所保留的人。

**Terravox:** Hah, I misjudged your resolve. I understand... This is the only choice.

> **荒笛：**呵，是我错估了你的决心。我明白…何为唯一的选择。

**Terravox:** Behold, this Coreflame that burns in my chest. This Coreflame born from the "Earth." It is the redsoil from which life sprouts, and the furnace of all living things...

> **荒笛：**看吧，我胸中燃烧的，这颗诞自「大地」的火种。它是生命萌芽的红土，万物的熔炉……

**Terravox:** It tells me to commit one final act of treason: Entrust this heavy task to the Trailblazers from beyond the sky. Let them tend to the wild terrain and safeguard the lands...

> **荒笛：**它告诉我，发起最后一次僭叛，将开垦荒土、守望大陆的重任交予天外的「开拓」……

**Terravox:** And thus bring the endless stream of life into the distant future.

> **荒笛：**如此，将生生不息者——带向远方。

**Dan Heng:** I promise you, Terravox.

> **丹恒：**我答应你，荒笛。

**Terravox:** Good. When I hand over the divine authority, this place will fall to pieces.

> **荒笛：**如此便好。神权交替的刹那，此地定将分崩离析……

**Terravox:** Are you prepared to hold up the Sacred Tree if it falls?

> **荒笛：**扶圣树之将倾，你可做好准备？

**Dan Heng:** Of course.

> **丹恒：**当然。

**Terravox:** Then free what remains of my body and let me give you this flame that turned away from the Flame-Chase—

> **荒笛：**那便解放吾之残躯，赠予你这枚背离逐火之火吧——

**Terravox:** I now relinquish my duty as a demigod.

> **荒笛：**我身为半神的职责，就于此刻将其卸下。

**Terravox:** "You shall dig your own grave and burn in the furnace of rebellion"...

> **荒笛：**「汝将自掘坟墓，焚毁于叛逆的熔炉」……

**Terravox:** I only hope that, after all this time, Father will take me back into their heart.

> **荒笛：**但愿父亲那久违的胸膛，接受我的回归。

**Dan Heng:** Come, crouching beast! For the endless life of this world...

> **丹恒：**来吧，伏地的巨兽：为此世生生不息——

**Dan Heng:** Rise from your dormancy, and follow me as soaring dragons of the wilderness!

> **丹恒：**从蛰伏中更生，随我作腾飞的荒龙！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Terravox:** Take it...

> **荒笛：**拿去吧……

**Terravox:** My final rebellion...

> **荒笛：**我最后的反叛……

**Terravox:** ...Then, declare to the "Earth" the name of the new god!

> **荒笛：**——然后，向「大地」昭告新神的名字！

**Dan Heng:** I am "Dan Heng"...

> **丹恒：**我是「丹恒」——

**Dan Heng:** The one who guards the Trailblaze's way forward!

> **丹恒：**护卫「开拓」前路之人——！

*(Cutscene ends)*

**"Dan Feng":** Fortunately...

> **「丹枫」：**所幸……

**"Dan Feng":** The worst did not come to pass.

> **「丹枫」：**最坏的结果，没有发生。

**"Dan Feng":** If your opponent had already turned into a vicious beast, whatever you did wouldn't weigh on your conscience... So what if that demigod had harbored ill intentions or vowed to fight to the death...

> **「丹枫」：**若对方已沦作害兽，正好免去你的心理负担——如果那位半神心怀不轨，或誓死不从……

**"Dan Feng":** Would you have pointed your spear at them and carved out the Coreflame with your own hands?

> **「丹枫」：**你会将枪尖指向它，亲手剜出这枚火种么？

**Dan Heng:** There's no point in discussing "what-ifs," though I am glad it didn't come to that.

> **丹恒：**讨论「如果」没有意义。但，我很庆幸，结局终究没有落到那一步。

**Dan Heng:** Regardless, I will attempt to join this game and become a part of the Era Nova.

> **丹恒：**无论如何，我都会试着以身入局，成为「再创世」的一部分。

**Dan Heng:** This is the only way I can face my friends and this world with a "clear conscience."

> **丹恒：**唯有如此，我才能对伙伴，还有这个世界，做到「问心无愧」。

**"Dan Feng":** Hah...

> **「丹枫」：**呵……

**"Dan Feng":** Now that things are settled between us, it's time for you and I to say goodbye.

> **「丹枫」：**既已了却分别心，就该是你我道别的时刻了。

**"Dan Feng":** Everything that I left behind has faded away at this point. Go and march toward your own "Permanence."

> **「丹枫」：**至此，我留下的一切尽数消散。迈向属于你的「不朽」吧。

**"Dan Feng":** As for how to tame it... I'll leave that to you.

> **「丹枫」：**至于如何驯服它，将是你的问题。

**Dan Heng:** Thank you. We can't go back to the past and make better choices. But at least we can do better in the future.

> **丹恒：**谢谢。我们无法回到过去，做出更好的选择。但至少，我们会在未来的轮回中做得更好。

**Dan Heng:** A dragon can only welcome new life by shedding away his old, scarred skin...

> **丹恒：**抛却遍体鳞伤的龙蜕，才算是迎来了新生……

**Dan Heng:** For the first time in my life, I feel at ease.

> **丹恒：**生来第一次，我感到如此轻松。

**"Dan Feng":** This burning Coreflame... Is this what the Destruction feels like?

> **「丹枫」：**燃烧的火种…这就是「毁灭」的滋味么？

**"Dan Feng":** The light is unexpectedly warm... It's just like...

> **「丹枫」：**这光芒，竟出乎意料地温暖…就像是……

**"Dan Feng":** The fleeting moments... when one wakes from a dream...

> **「丹枫」：**每一次…从梦中醒来的片刻……

**Dan Heng:** Farewell, Dan Feng...

> **丹恒：**别了，丹枫……

**Dan Heng:** Now... it's time for me to set out once more.

> **丹恒：**现在，也该是我重新启程的时刻了。

### Talk with Hysilens

> *The authority of Earth is now in my hands. I will shoulder this responsibility and stand with you all to face "Era Nova." If it were Terravox and Evernight who built the memoria tide world, then I now have the power to bring (Trailblazer) back.*

**"Hysilens":** The final rebellion of "Earth" was to bestow Trailblazing wings upon all living beings...

> **「海瑟音」：**「大地」的最后一次反叛，是为世间生灵插上「开拓」的羽翼……

**"Hysilens":** For Terravox, this was probably the best possible ending.

> **「海瑟音」：**对荒笛来说，这应该是最好的结局。

**"Hysilens":** Behind that scar lies what they called the "deepest depths." The rising steam tells me that (Trailblazer) is in there...

> **「海瑟音」：**那道伤痕背后，就是它口中的「至深之地」。热汽告诉我，开拓者就在其中……

**"Hysilens":** But right now, he/she's trapped in a bottomless whirlpool.

> **「海瑟音」：**但此刻，他/她正陷于一片深不见底的漩涡。

**Dan Heng:** Is "Evernight" with him/her?

> **丹恒：**「长夜月」在他/她身边么？

**"Hysilens":** I'm afraid I can't tell.

> **「海瑟音」：**抱歉，我难以判断。

**Dan Heng:** A scorching abyss... If I were to enter it alone, I'd almost certainly get lost.

> **丹恒：**炙热的深渊…若我一人贸然踏足其中，迷失在所难免。

**Dan Heng:** Miss Hysilens, would you give me cover? I'll need to focus completely on what's ahead and may not be able to pay attention to my surroundings.

> **丹恒：**海瑟音女士，可否请你为我掩护？接下来，我必须全神贯注，恐怕无暇顾及周遭。

**"Hysilens":** What are you planning to do?

> **「海瑟音」：**要做什么？

**Dan Heng:** I'm going to continue where you left off.

> **丹恒：**延续你先前的尝试。

**"Hysilens":** The stranger snatched away the "Deliverer" right before my eyes. I commanded the waters to surge after them, but layered cliffs blocked the water's current...

> **「海瑟音」：**那位陌生人在我眼前掠走了「救世主」。我号令众水奔涌追逐，却被层岩阻断了流向……

**Dan Heng:** Times have changed... The cliffs... are now in the palm of my hand.

> **丹恒：**今时不同往日，层岩…已尽在我的掌心。

**"Hysilens":** Then I believe it's worth a shot.

> **「海瑟音」：**我相信，这值得一试。

**"Hysilens":** It's just... without absolute resolve, you still won't be able to pass through that memoria tide, much less save someone from it.

> **「海瑟音」：**只是…若无万分坚定之心，你依旧会受到那片忆潮阻拦，更遑论从中打捞同伴。

**"Hysilens":** The search for (Trailblazer) might stretch on like an evernight without end... Be careful. Wandering through "Time" is no easy feat.

> **「海瑟音」：**寻找开拓者的过程，也许会漫长如永夜…务必小心，在「岁月」中彷徨，绝非易事。

**Dan Heng:** Don't worry, Lady Hysilens. Just as you kept your lonely vigil in the depths for a thousand years, my determination to find (Trailblazer)...

> **丹恒：**不用担心，海瑟音女士。正如你在海底独守千年，我找回开拓者的决心……

**Dan Heng:** Is even more unshakeable than the "Earth" itself.

> **丹恒：**也比「大地」更坚不可摧。

### Find (Trailblazer) and bring him/her home.

**Dan Heng:** Ancient Sacred Tree, lend me your roots. With the Coreflame I carry, let me resonate with this land.

> **丹恒：**古老的圣树，将你的根系借给我吧。用我背负的火种，与这片陆地共鸣。

**Dan Heng:** With heaven and earth as my map and time as my compass, I will find that one singular point.

> **丹恒：**以天地为横轴，以时光为纵轴，我将找到那唯一的一点。

**Dan Heng:** Even if I must cross every mountain and valley on my own two feet...

> **丹恒：**哪怕要用我的双脚，遍历这山川大地的每一处角落……

## 155　Great Tomb, Hide the Secrets of Incarnations　大墓啊，深藏轮回的秘密

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Radiant Scarwood" Grove of Epiphany　|　角色：Trailblazer; Dan Heng • Permansor Terrae; Castorice; Hysilens; Cyrene; Evey; Evernight; Black Swan; March 7th　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Earth, Bear the Suffering of All'' completed

### Talk with Dan Heng

> *Dan Heng, your most trusted Express Guard, and the most reliable companion on your Trailblaze journey. He accepted Earth's divine authority, solely to stand alongside you and the Chrysos Heir friends, facing the new world and the impending final clash together. Just knowing he's back in Amphoreus gives you not just one, but a hundred times more peace of mind. When his consciousness delves into the memoria tide where no one else can reach, he is the bedrock, the waves, and the frosty gales... He is the Earth and all that exists upon it. He came for you, and now you will both go for March. The blazing sun has shown the way. Press onward.*

**Dan Heng:** Finally...

> **丹恒：**终于……

**Dan Heng:** I've found you, (Trailblazer).

> **丹恒：**开拓者，我找到你了。

- ▸ Dan Heng, it's been a while...

> ▸ 丹恒，好久不见…

**(Trailblazer):** It really has... been a long time.

> **开拓者：**真的…好久不见了。

**Dan Heng:** Don't be so sentimental. That's not like you.

> **丹恒：**别那么感伤啊，这不像你。

- ▸ Dan Heng, did you... get taller?

> ▸ 丹恒，你…长高了？

**(Trailblazer):** Long time no see. Did you... get taller?

> **开拓者：**好久不见，你…长高了？

**Dan Heng:** Heh. While you were trapped, I experienced... a lot.

> **丹恒：**嗯。你被困住的时候，我经历了…许多事。

- ▸ I've been waiting for you for so long, Dan Heng.

> ▸ 丹恒，等你好久了。

**(Trailblazer):** Dan Heng, I knew... you would come back.

> **开拓者：**丹恒，我知道…你会回来的。

**Dan Heng:** Heh. While you were trapped, I experienced... a lot.

> **丹恒：**嗯。你被困住的时候，我经历了…许多事。

**Dan Heng:** Thankfully, "Evernight" did not try to stop me. Looks like we've managed to stay ahead of her this time.

> **丹恒：**万幸，「长夜月」没有出手阻拦。看来这一次，我们成功抢占了先机。

- ▸ We must save March 7th.

> ▸ 必须救回三月七。

**Dan Heng:** Rest assured, we won't abandon her. We'll all return to the Express together... No one will be left behind.

> **丹恒：**放心，没有人会放弃她。我们会一起返回列车…一个不落。

- ▸ What about Cyrene? Where is she?

> ▸ 昔涟…她在哪里？

**Dan Heng:** You mean "Mem"? Sorry, I haven't seen her at all on this journey. Perhaps you were not the only one being held captive by "Evernight."

> **丹恒：**你指「迷迷」吗？抱歉，这一路上我都没遇见她。恐怕被「长夜月」囚禁的，不止你一人。

- ▸ How's the Express Crew doing?

> ▸ 列车组还好吗？

**Dan Heng:** They never once stopped worrying about us, about you, me, or March 7th. They never gave up on trying to save us, even from outside this world.

> **丹恒：**他们一直心系你、我，还有三月七的安危。即便身处世界外，也从未停下过救援。

**Dan Heng:** Miss Himeko, Mr. Yang, Pom-Pom, and even Sunday, Black Swan, and the two geniuses... They are all doing everything in their power to aid us.

> **丹恒：**姬子小姐、瓦尔特先生、帕姆，还有星期日、黑天鹅、两位天才…所有人都在尽己所能相助。

- ▸ She was everywhere in the memoria tide...

> ▸ 那片忆潮中，她无处不在……

**Dan Heng:** I understand. Her power... shouldn't be underestimated.

> **丹恒：**我明白。她的力量…不容小觑。

**Dan Heng:** It was only when I personally stepped into the maze of "Time" that it suddenly struck me... She and I had crossed paths once before.

> **丹恒：**亲身踏入那座「岁月」的迷宫时，我才猛然想起…自己和她早有过一面之缘。

**Dan Heng:** I'll only warn you once. Leave her body. Now.

> **丹恒：**我只警告一次——立刻，离开她的身体。

**Dan Heng:** I feel like I've seen these floating black things somewhere before...

> **丹恒：**这些黑色的浮游物，我仿佛在哪里见过……

**Dan Heng:** I had an unexpected encounter with her when I left Amphoreus. Perhaps to avoid exposing her whereabouts, she stripped that memory from my mind.

> **丹恒：**离开翁法罗斯时，我无意中和她产生过接触。或许是不想暴露行踪，她从我的脑海中剥离了那段记忆。

**Dan Heng:** That power called "Oblivion"... it's just like an Emanator's.

> **丹恒：**那股名为「忘却」的力量…与令使无异。

**(Trailblazer):** (Oblivion...)

> **开拓者：**（忘却……）

- ▸ She wants to erase "Worldbearing's" memories.

> ▸ 她想要清洗「负世」的记忆。

- ▸ She planned to make me forget everything...

> ▸ 她计划让我遗忘一切…

**Dan Heng:** I know. But you still remember me and our companions for now.

> **丹恒：**我知道。但现在，你还记得我，记得我们的伙伴。

**Dan Heng:** Do you have any clues about the memories you may have lost?

> **丹恒：**对于可能丢失的记忆，你有任何头绪吗？

- ▸ Nothing seems out of the ordinary...

> ▸ 似乎没什么异常…

**Dan Heng:** If I'm not mistaken, Evernight's plan is to make you forget Amphoreus, including the Chrysos Heirs who have journeyed with us up till now.

> **丹恒：**如果我没猜错，按照长夜月的计划，她打算让你遗忘翁法罗斯，包括与我们同行至今的黄金裔。

- ▸ "Mere mortals. Flawed individuals. Failed heroes..."

> ▸ 「凡人、缺陷者、失败的英雄」…

**Dan Heng:** Are those "Evernight's" exact words? If I'm not mistaken, she intends to make you forget Amphoreus, including the Chrysos Heirs who have journeyed with us up till now.

> **丹恒：**这是「长夜月」的原话么…如果没理解错，她打算让你遗忘翁法罗斯，包括与我们同行至今的黄金裔。

**Dan Heng:** But it seems you still recognize Lady Hysilens.

> **丹恒：**可看上去，你也还认识这位海瑟音女士。

**"Hysilens":** If I made that much of an impression on Little Gray Fry, then I am naturally delighted.

> **「海瑟音」：**倘若是小灰鱼儿对我印象深刻，我自然欣喜。

**"Hysilens":** But I fear your escape from that power they call "Oblivion"...

> **「海瑟音」：**但恐怕，你能从那「忘却」的力量中幸免……

**"Hysilens":** Is thanks to someone else.

> **「海瑟音」：**另有一位功臣。

- ▸ Castorice...?

> ▸ 遐蝶…？

- ▸ Was it you... who protected me?

> ▸ 是你…保护了我？

**"Castorice":** Nice to meet you, or... maybe I should ask, how have you been since we last saw each other?

> **「遐蝶」：**初次见面，或者…别来无恙，阁下。

**"Hysilens":** Along with you, this little butterflyfish's memory was also salvaged from the depths of that whirlpool.

> **「海瑟音」：**方才，从漩涡深处被一同打捞上来的，还有这道小蝶鱼的记忆。

**"Hysilens":** Just as a pearl is cradled within its shell... the power of Thanatos surrounds you, shielding your soul from corrosion.

> **「海瑟音」：**恰如蚌壳之于明珠…塞纳托斯的权能包裹着你，使你的灵魂免受侵蚀。

**"Castorice":** Miss Cyrene has spoken of you often to me, (Trailblazer). Though we never met in this life, I believe... there's a kind of warmth that can transcend time itself.

> **「遐蝶」：**昔涟小姐和我反复提起过你，开拓者阁下。尽管此世我们未能相逢，但我相信…有一种温度足以跨越时间。

**"Castorice":** Through the endless flow of time, I guarded the secret passage between Styxia and the Vortex. When the memoria tide flowed backwards into the River of Souls, even in deep slumber, I could feel that warmth...

> **「遐蝶」：**漫长的时光里，我守护着斯缇科西亚与涡心的密道。当逆流的忆潮渗入冥河，即便深陷沉睡，我也能感受到那一丝温暖……

**"Castorice":** And with all my might, I protected that warmth.

> **「遐蝶」：**并倾尽全力，将其呵护。

**"Castorice":** I apologize if that seemed improper. I hope you'll forgive me.

> **「遐蝶」：**此举或有失礼节，还请阁下见谅。

- ▸ Thank you, Castorice.

> ▸ 谢谢你，遐蝶。

- ▸ In the end, you were there for me.

> ▸ 无论如何，你保护了我。

- ▸ See, I really am a heartwarming guy/girl deep down.

> ▸ 我的拥抱果然很温暖嘛。

**Dan Heng:** She was just speaking metaphorically... This conversation feels familiar somehow.

> **丹恒：**她只是在用比喻…这番对话好像在哪里见过。

**"Castorice":** Haha...

> **「遐蝶」：**哈哈……

**"Castorice":** I understand that your mission is still unfinished. The path ahead is treacherous, and even though my body is bound to these dark currents, I'll do everything I can to watch over you both.

> **「遐蝶」：**我知道，阁下还有未竟的使命。此行艰险，即便身躯囿于溟流，我也会竭力看护二位的去路。

**"Hysilens":** The little butterflyfish is right. There is still a pair of eyes watching from the shadows, but we won't let them get anywhere near you.

> **「海瑟音」：**小蝶鱼说得没错，仍有一双眼睛在暗中眈视——但我们不会给他从背后接近二位的机会。

**"Hysilens":** Go forth, golden dragonfish, and you, "Deliverer," seek out your companion...

> **「海瑟音」：**前进吧，金色的龙鱼，还有「救世主」，去寻找你们的同伴……

**"Hysilens":** Go forth and illuminate the lightless "Evernight."

> **「海瑟音」：**去照亮那无光的「长夜」。

**Dan Heng:** I cannot thank you enough. Accept this promise from the Nameless: Amphoreus will definitely write a brand-new ending for itself.

> **丹恒：**感激不尽。请收下无名客的承诺：翁法罗斯，一定能为自己写下崭新的结局。

**"Castorice":** Of course, we'll write it together.

> **「遐蝶」：**当然，我们会共同写就。

- ▸ Thank you, Hysilens.

> ▸ 谢谢你，海瑟音。

- ▸ Thank you too, Castorice.

> ▸ 也谢谢你，遐蝶。

**(Trailblazer):** The Nameless will never stop...

> **开拓者：**无名客的脚步不会停下……

**Dan Heng:** Until all of us stand side by side in the true new world.

> **丹恒：**直到我们所有人，在真正的新世界并肩。
