**"Evernight":** We meet again, naive "me."

> **「长夜月」：**又见面了，天真的「我」。

**"March 7th":** I know you mean that as a compliment...

> **「三月七」：**虽然知道你是诚心诚意在夸我……

**"March 7th":** But as for the word "naive"... I think I'll give you that one back.

> **「三月七」：**但「天真」这两个字，还是还给你吧？

**Cyrene:** Welcome to Aedes Elysiae, a small village shrouded by the Veil of Evernight. Now, this memory will also belong to you...

> **昔涟：**欢迎来到哀丽秘榭，永夜之帷包围的小村庄。现在，这道回忆也属于你……

**Cyrene:** Let's give you a taste of your own medicine. With my home as the inspiration, I've created this "maze"... Hope you like it, Miss Evernight.

> **昔涟：**以彼之道，还治彼身。我以故乡为灵感打造的「迷宫」，长夜月小姐，希望你喜欢。

**"Evernight":** Quite perceptive, little memosprite. But with just you and that Harmony's little birdie... thinking you could trap me? That's a bit of a stretch.

> **「长夜月」：**悟性很高呢，小忆灵。但只凭你和「同谐」的小鸟——想困住我，多少有些勉强了呀？

**"Evernight":** So, tell me, what role did "you" play in all of this?

> **「长夜月」：**所以，和我说说吧，「你」又在其中发挥了什么作用？

**"March 7th":** I really didn't think that... this other "me" would be THIS strong...

> **「三月七」：**是啊，真没想到…另一个「我」居然强得这么不像话……

**"March 7th":** But at least when it comes to brains, I've still got the edge! My brilliant plan? Bet you never saw it coming!

> **「三月七」：**还好还好，至少在「头脑」方面，咱还算占了上风！本姑娘的绝妙计策，完全超出你的想象。

**"Evernight":** ...

> **「长夜月」：**……

**"Evernight":** Oh... you mean that time?

> **「长夜月」：**喔…是那个时候？

**"Evernight":** In that split second when we switched bodies... with your last flicker of consciousness...

> **「长夜月」：**在你我易换身体的瞬间，你用稍纵即逝的最后一丝心识……

**"Evernight":** You hid yourself within the Titan's veil.

> **「长夜月」：**将自己藏进了泰坦的帷幕啊。

**"March 7th":** Huh? You got it already? No suspense at all?

> **「三月七」：**呀，怎么一下子就被猜出来了…真没悬念！

**"March 7th":** But my plan was super thought-out! I know (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng better than anyone. A promise is a promise for them. They never go back on their word!

> **「三月七」：**但我的计划可一点也不脆弱！没人比我更懂开拓者和丹恒。既然约好了，他俩就不会食言——

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Cyrene:** History books, diaries, and photostones are all perfect catalysts for "memories."

> **昔涟：**无论史书、日记，还是留影石机，都是「记忆」绝佳的触媒。

**"March 7th":** That's right! So when I hear (Trailblazer) press the shutter... even if there's just a teeny bit of my consciousness left...

> **「三月七」：**没错！当我听见开拓者按下快门的声音，就算精神只留下一点点残余……

**"March 7th":** I'd "whoosh" right into the camera!

> **「三月七」：**也会「咻」地一下，自己钻进相机里去！

**Cyrene:** But if you put it like that... Aren't you leaving a lot to chance?

> **昔涟：**可这样看来，巧合的成分…是不是还挺大的？

**"March 7th":** No way! (Trailblazer) will definitely be snapping away all over Amphoreus because he/she promised me to take photos! I'm guaranteed to run into him/her eventually.

> **「三月七」：**怎么会！既然说了要给我拍照，开拓者肯定会在翁法罗斯四处按下快门。我总有机会等到他/她。

**Cyrene:** True, you really know him/her well.

> **昔涟：**你确实很了解他/她呀。

**Cyrene:** Miss Evernight, let's be real, my amateur skills aren't enough to keep someone like you contained.

> **昔涟：**「长夜月」小姐，以我现学现卖的本事，想困住你当然不现实。

**Cyrene:** But if "March 7th" were here, that would be a different story...

> **昔涟：**但如果「三月七」也在场，局面就不一样了……

**Cyrene:** Because there's no way for you to ignore her, right?

> **昔涟：**因为你无论如何都无法忽视她，对吧？

**"Evernight":** ...

> **「长夜月」：**……

**"Evernight":** Fine. Lay it on me then. After going on such a long detour, what exactly do you want to do now?

> **「长夜月」：**可以，那让我拭目以待。绕了这么远的路，你接下来打算怎么做？

**"March 7th":** First, I want to thank you for protecting (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng. But what you did later went way too far...

> **「三月七」：**首先，我要谢谢你保护了开拓者和丹恒。但后来，你做的事就太过火了……

**"March 7th":** So, while there's still time, I've got to make you change your mind!

> **「三月七」：**趁一切还来得及，我必须让你回心转意才行！

**"Evernight":** There IS something that truly puzzles me...

> **「长夜月」：**有一件事的确令我困惑……

**"Evernight":** How did you find yourself again after giving up all your "memories" and becoming an empty soul?

> **「长夜月」：**在献出一切「记忆」，化作空无的精魄后，你是如何找回自我的？

**"March 7th":** Looks like you aren't so omnipotent or omniscient after all, huh?

> **「三月七」：**看来你也不是全知全能嘛？

**"March 7th":** The answer is simple. "I" have been dozing inside the camera all along, and I accompanied (Trailblazer) during his/her entire journey.

> **「三月七」：**答案很简单：这一路上，「我」一直沉睡在相机里，陪着开拓者走完了全程。

**"March 7th":** So when he/she told Cyrene in this cycle about his/her interstellar travels, those memories of journeying with the Express entered my mind as well...

> **「三月七」：**而这一世，在他/她和昔涟讲述星际旅行的时候，那些与列车同行的回忆也钻入了我的脑海……

**"March 7th":** I may still be far from the "complete March 7th"... but these "memories" were more than enough to fight you.

> **「三月七」：**虽然还远远称不上「完整的三月七」，但用来和你对峙…这些「记忆」足够了。

**"Evernight":** ...Hah.

> **「长夜月」：**…呵。

**"Evernight":** So, you know what led up to this and what followed, right?

> **「长夜月」：**所以，前因后果你都知道，对吗？

**"Evernight":** Go ahead. Tell me. Let's hear why you're so confident you can sway me.

> **「长夜月」：**那就说说看吧，你有信心说服我的理由。

**"March 7th":** Why? Isn't the reason super obvious?

> **「三月七」：**理由什么的，不是再简单不过了吗？

**"March 7th":** Even through a camera lens, I know that the love, hate, and struggles that happened in Amphoreus were the same as what real living people experience.

> **「三月七」：**哪怕只是透过镜头，我也知道，发生在翁法罗斯的爱、恨、挣扎，跟活生生的人没有区别。

**"Evernight":** Same old topic. I already got (Trailblazer) to make a choice.

> **「长夜月」：**话题又绕回了原点呢，我已经让开拓者做过一次选择了。

**"Evernight":** The weights on each side of the scale are far too unequal. When Destruction looms, trying to preserve everything... only means sacrificing everything.

> **「长夜月」：**天秤两端的配重，相差太过悬殊。在「毁灭」的威胁面前，追求两全其美…只会两害得兼。

**"March 7th":** Well, take a step back. Before we ask this question...

> **「三月七」：**可是，在提出这个问题前……

**"March 7th":** Have you ever really thought about it? What right do we really have... to make choices for others?

> **「三月七」：**你有想过吗？我们…有什么资格替别人做出决定呢？

**"March 7th":** If the cosmos were nothing more than a bigger Okhema, ruled by a so-called "Imperator"... maybe then she'd have the right to weigh both sides and decide for us all.

> **「三月七」：**假如银河是一座更大的奥赫玛，里头住着一位「凯撒」，那她也许可以替所有人做主，称量天秤两端的重量。

**"March 7th":** But the truth is... We're just a bunch of Nameless.

> **「三月七」：**但别忘了，我们只是一群「无名客」——

**"March 7th":** Even with everyone calling him/her "Deliverer," I believe that (Trailblazer) hasn't forgotten...

> **「三月七」：**就算被人口口声声「救世主」、「救世主」地叫着，我相信开拓者也没有忘记过……

**"March 7th":** That the meaning of Trailblaze is to "explore, understand, establish, and connect." It's not about standing above the cosmos as some kind of savior. It's about walking alongside every world within it.

> **「三月七」：**「开拓」的意义是「探索、了解、建立、连结」，是与无数世界同行，而不是高高在上的「拯救银河」。

**"Evernight":** ...

> **「长夜月」：**……

**"March 7th":** So don't try to guilt-trip me with stuff like "necessary sacrifices." The Express Crew is immune to these baseless accusations!

> **「三月七」：**所以，别想用什么「牺牲在所难免」来绑架我，列车组对这些毫无根据的指责是免疫的！

**"March 7th":** Besides — and this is just an example — if Madam Herta wanted to, she could have whipped out her Imaginary weapon any time and blasted this "Scepter" to bits.

> **「三月七」：**况且——我只是打个比方——只要黑塔女士愿意，她随时可以掏出虚数武器，把这台「权杖」炸个灰飞烟灭。

**"March 7th":** Because, according to your logic, the lives of a few Nameless are nothing compared to a Lord Ravager's potential threat.

> **「三月七」：**用你的话说，跟一位绝灭大君可能造成的威胁相比，区区几个无名客的命又算得上什么呢？

**"March 7th":** But that's not what she did. You wouldn't have liked it either, would you? I figured you out a long time ago... you've got a fatal flaw.

> **「三月七」：**但她没有这么做，而你，也不会同意这件事，对吧？我早就发现了，你也有一项致命的弱点……

**"March 7th":** A weakness in the shape of "me."

> **「三月七」：**那就是「我」。

**"March 7th":** You've got such incredible powers, but you still kept your promise to me. That only means...

> **「三月七」：**明明拥有这么蛮不讲理的力量，却还是遵守了对我的承诺。我只能认为……

**"March 7th":** The "sole motivation" behind everything you do was just so "I" can keep travelling.

> **「三月七」：**你所有行动的「原动力」，只是为了让「我」能继续旅行下去。

**"Evernight":** Hah...

> **「长夜月」：**呵……

**"Evernight":** You're... not as naive as you seem, are you?

> **「长夜月」：**你好像，确实没有那么天真呢。

**"March 7th":** In that case, I can prove it to you.

> **「三月七」：**既然如此，我可以向你证明。

**"March 7th":** I won't answer for someone else, but for "myself."

> **「三月七」：**不是替别人——而是替「自己」给出回答。

[Recap] March 7th showed Evernight her journal made of her spirit, filled with photos from the camera. This was the "memories" and also March 7th's "Trailblaze" journey all along.

**"March 7th":** Here, hold this.

> **「三月七」：**来——抱着它。

**"Evernight":** An empty spirit...

> **「长夜月」：**空无的精魄……

**"March 7th":** If you want to seriously think about it, this thing actually counts as my body right now.

> **「三月七」：**细究的话，现在它才算是我的本体。

**"March 7th":** Its memories are all drawn from the camera's photos, in other words, from my journey of Trailblazing.

> **「三月七」：**构成它的记忆，全部来自相机中的照片，也就是我一路以来的「开拓」。

**"March 7th":** While I was awake, you were always sleeping, so talking about this probably doesn't feel real to you...

> **「三月七」：**我醒着的时候，你一直都在沉睡。泛泛而谈，在你听来多半没有实感……

**"March 7th":** So, why don't I walk you through these memories all over again?

> **「三月七」：**所以现在，我带你重新回忆一遍吧？

**"Evernight":** The spirit... turned into a journal?

> **「长夜月」：**精魄，变成了一本手账？

**"March 7th":** Yep. That's what "memories" are.

> **「三月七」：**没错，这就是「记忆」。

**"March 7th":** You don't know where they are, and you don't know what you actually remember. But when you need them most, the forces that have once changed your soul...

> **「三月七」：**你不清楚它存在于哪里，也不知道留下的是哪些。但在最需要它的时候，那些改变过你心灵的力量……

**"March 7th":** Are guaranteed to appear again.

> **「三月七」：**一定会再度浮现。

[Recap] On the night before the warp jump, March 7th shared a souvenir notebook from Belobog with you and Dan Heng. She encouraged you both to practice photography while she recorded the photos in her journal.

Astral Express, the night before warping to Xianzhou Luofu...

**March 7th:** So, (Trailblazer)? How was your first Trailblaze journey?

> **三月七：**…怎么样，开拓者？第一次「开拓」之旅，感觉如何？

**(Trailblazer):** ...

> **开拓者：**……

**(Trailblazer):** It was very fun.

> **开拓者：**很有趣。

**Dan Heng:** We should still be careful in the future.

> **丹恒：**以后还是得注意安全。

**March 7th:** Oh come on, Dan Heng, we're here to celebrate for (Trailblazer) tonight. Don't be such a buzzkill!

> **三月七：**哎呀，丹恒老师，咱们今晚是来给开拓者庆祝的，就别说这种扫兴话啦。

**March 7th:** Don't listen to him. Listen, I am practically a "Trailblaze" expert. Stick with me, and you'll be just fine~

> **三月七：**别听他吓唬人。跟你讲，本姑娘也算半个「开拓」专家——跟着咱走，准没事～

**Dan Heng:** You call this trip "fine"?

> **丹恒：**你管这趟叫「没事」吗？

**March 7th:** W-well, that's because we could only find 700-year-old guides online!

> **三月七：**那、那还不是因为网上只能搜到七百年前的攻略！

**(Trailblazer):** So... you've been to a lot of places before?

> **开拓者：**看来…你们去过很多地方了？

**March 7th:** You bet! Capital of Passion, Kalsas, Taikiyan... If I count on my hand, it looks like this is my sixth stop.

> **三月七：**那是当然：热夜之都、凯尔萨斯星、泰科铵…掰掰手指，这该是我的第六站啦。

**March 7th:** All my souvenirs are in my room, but they're just... a bit of a mess right now. I'll tidy up later and give you the grand tour!

> **三月七：**纪念品都在房间里呢，不过没来得及收拾。回头整理好了，再给你好好炫耀吧。

**March 7th:** Look at this. A Belobogian notebook, a special souvenir I asked Bronya for!

> **三月七：**看——贝洛伯格的笔记本，我特地问布洛妮娅要的纪念品！

**March 7th:** She prepared quite a few gifts for us! Raw Geomarrow, Supreme Guardian medals... but I only asked for this little notebook.

> **三月七：**她给咱们准备了好些礼物呢：地髓原矿、大守护者奖章…不过，我只要了这个小本本。

**(Trailblazer):** What's so special about it?

> **开拓者：**它有什么特别的？

**March 7th:** The previous Trailblaze missions were all on super high-tech worlds. Forget about paper books. Even holographic projections were ancient relics there.

> **三月七：**先前几次「开拓」，去的尽是些高科技世界——别说纸书了，就连全息投影都算老古董。

**March 7th:** Jarilo is completely different. Watching people record their lives with pen and paper... I finally understood why some still cherish writing journals by hand.

> **三月七：**雅利洛就很不一样，看着大家用纸笔记录生活，我才明白为什么会有人还在坚持制作手账。

**Dan Heng:** It's just like "calligraphy." Those deep emotions buried in human hearts can only be awakened through such seemingly thankless actions.

> **丹恒：**就像「书法」。深藏于人心底的感动，只有靠这种看似吃力不讨好的方式才能唤醒。

**March 7th:** Dan Heng... you truly embody both a fierce beast and a delicate rose!

> **三月七：**丹恒老师…还真是心有猛虎，细嗅蔷薇！

**March 7th:** Anyways, that's what I thought too. A camera can only capture fleeting moments, but stitching those moments into a story? You've gotta use your own hands...

> **三月七：**但咱也这么想的。相机只能拍下瞬间的风景，但要把那些切片连缀成人生，还是得靠自己的双手才行……

**March 7th:** In other words, just taking photos isn't enough. They need a place to be displayed!

> **三月七：**简单来说，光拍照不行，相片总要有个地方供人展示嘛~

**(Trailblazer):** Isn't the photo wall enough?

> **开拓者：**有照片墙还不够吗？

**March 7th:** I told you it's different! Besides, this little notebook is a memento of our first Trailblaze together with (Trailblazer)! That makes it even more special.

> **三月七：**都说了不一样。而且，这个小本本是咱和开拓者第一次共同「开拓」的纪念品！意义可就更重大了，嘿嘿。

**March 7th:** Anyways, the next stop is the legendary Planet of Festivities, Penacony...

> **三月七：**这么说来，下一站就是传说中大名鼎鼎的「盛会之星」，匹诺康尼……

**March 7th:** So when we get there, I'll make sure we take tons of pictures together, until the memory card's bursting!

> **三月七：**到时候，我可要使劲拉着你们合影，把储存卡都塞满！

**Dan Heng:** Fine by me.

> **丹恒：**我没意见。

**March 7th:** And one more thing. If I'm ever too busy to get off at a stop, this camera is gonna be in your hands.

> **「三月七」：**独自度过的那97天，可把我紧张得不行，以为这辈子都要交代在这儿了……

**March 7th:** I don't want to miss a single "Trailblaze." So you all need to practice your photography skills until you're at least 80% as good as me, got it?

> **「三月七」：**还好，本姑娘胆大心细、足智多谋，才没落在开拓者和丹恒身后……

[Recap] Hidden within the camera, March 7th had traveled alongside you the entire time. Through exploring, understanding, establishing, and connecting, her name too became part of the Hero's Journey known as "Amphoreus."

**"March 7th":** Back then, I was only half-joking. I never thought I'd actually miss out... and after just two stops, too.

**"March 7th":** Those 97 days alone had me on edge. I honestly thought that was gonna be it for me...

**"March 7th":** Thankfully, with my quick thinking and sharp planning, I managed to keep up with (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng.

**"Evernight":** By hiding in the camera?

> **「长夜月」：**以藏在相机里的形式？

**"March 7th":** So what? That's still "Trailblazing"!

> **「三月七」：**那怎么啦？那也算是「开拓」！

**"March 7th":** "The camera is my true form" That might've been a joke, but it came true this time!

> **「三月七」：**「照相机才是我的本体」——这句玩笑话，这次不就成真了嘛？

**"March 7th":** Well, yeah, it was super inconvenient. I couldn't help them when they were in danger, couldn't complain when they talked total nonsense... and watching (Trailblazer) snap a terrible photo? I couldn't even control the shutter!

> **「三月七」：**虽然是很不方便啦：遇见危险没法出手；有人说怪话没法吐槽；看见开拓者拍烂片，我甚至都没法控制快门……

**"March 7th":** But I stuck with them all the way, til where we are now...

> **「三月七」：**但无论如何，我还是和他们一路走到了现在——

**"March 7th":** Together with them, I got to know this beautiful yet dying world. I claimed our first Coreflame on the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **「三月七」：**和大家一起认识这个濒毁却美丽的世界，为逐火之旅夺得第一枚火种——

**"March 7th":** I met new companions, then bid them farewell.

> **「三月七」：**和新的伙伴相遇，又与新的伙伴离别——

**"March 7th":** I inherited everyone's determination, carrying it forward into the future they fought for, one they can never see.

> **「三月七」：**继承大家的决心，将其带向他们为之牺牲了一切，却又无缘看见的未来——

**"March 7th":** Until my destiny becomes one with this world's, rewriting the predestined tragic ending and returning to the true starry skies together with everyone.

> **「三月七」：**直到和这个世界的命运融为一体，改写注定走向悲剧的结局，和所有人一起重返真正的星空——

**"March 7th":** Only then can I pen the name "March 7th"...

> **「三月七」：**只有这样，我才能在这场名为「翁法罗斯」的英雄之旅中……

**"March 7th":** Into this Hero's Journey called "Amphoreus"... Into this unprecedented Trailblaze!

> **「三月七」：**在这场前所未有的伟大「开拓」中，也写下「三月七」的名字！

33,550,336th eternal recurrence, Light Calendar 4932...

Sometime before the campaign against Aquila...

**Dan Heng:** Looks like our Trailblaze in Amphoreus is coming to an end.

> **丹恒：**看来，我们在翁法罗斯的「开拓」，也要临近尾声了。

**(Trailblazer):** While we still have time, shall we pick out some materials for March's journal?

> **丹恒：**…我不同意，但你请便。我只是觉得，手账还是该以记录风土人情为主。

> **开拓者：**不愧是我！啊对了，这张如何：永夜之帷，欧洛尼斯，藏在迷雾背后的泰坦…够震撼吧？

> **开拓者：**哎呀，当时那场面多紧张，我只能抓拍，有就不错了。

> **「三月七」：**……

> **开拓者：**咦…？

> **「三月七」：**他们一直都在我身边。没人知道我被遗忘的过去，也没人会心有芥蒂。因为……

**"Evernight":** ...

> **「长夜月」：**……

**"Evernight":** I still remember the day when you first put on these clothes and looked in the mirror.

> **「长夜月」：**我没有忘记，你第一次换上这身衣服，看向镜子的那天。

**"Evernight":** Your eyes were so clear. When all this is over, I hope the mirror can still reflect those same eyes.

> **「长夜月」：**你的眼睛很清澈。当一切过去，我希望镜子映出的，依旧是那双眼眸。

> **「长夜月」：**……

**Cyrene:** The Imperator added one extra rule to the final Era Nova at the cost of her life. That's how she can ensure Amphoreus doesn't become a sacrifice in the cosmic conflict.

> **昔涟：**凯撒以自己的性命为代价，为最后的「再创世」添加了一道规则：如此，刻律德菈才能确保翁法罗斯不会成为银河对垒的牺牲品——

**Cyrene:** Even if this world has to exist in the form of Irontomb, it will stand on its own among the stars, just as she had wanted.

> **昔涟：**就算只能以铁墓的形式，这个世界也能如她所想那样，自立于星间。

**"Evernight":** A daring wager indeed... That ruthless Imperator. Could she have already sensed that the Remembrance was plotting in the shadows?

**Cyrene:** But if you insist on doing this right now, then the cosmos will undoubtedly face the worst possible outcome...

> **昔涟：**但现在，如果你执意要这么做，银河无疑会落入最糟糕的结局……

**Cyrene:** (Trailblazer), Dan Heng, and March 7th will all merge with the Scepter and become a victim of the Destruction.

> **昔涟：**开拓者、丹恒，还有三月七，都将与权杖合为一体，坠入「毁灭」。

**"Evernight":** Hah...

> **「长夜月」：**呵……

**"Evernight":** You... knew all along?

> **「长夜月」：**你…一直都知道？

**"March 7th":** Yep. But I decided I needed to have a heart-to-heart with you first.

> **「三月七」：**嗯。但我还是决定，要先和你把心里话说开才行。

> **「三月七」：**即便内在是一片「长夜」，我也不会害怕。

**"March 7th":** That's because my journey's destination lies among the stars. I believe what lies ahead will be bright enough to illuminate my past.

> **「三月七」：**因为此行的终点是群星，我相信，前方的风景足以将昨天照亮。

**"March 7th":** Thank you for your... coddling, but please trust me and leave it to me. Both my past and that obsessive "Oblivion"....

> **「三月七」：**谢谢你的…溺爱。但现在，请安心把它交给我吧。无论是我的过去，还是那道「忘却」的执念……

**"March 7th":** Will help me become even stronger than (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng!

> **「三月七」：**它们都能帮助我，变得比开拓者和丹恒更强！

**"Evernight":** I'm sorry for becoming an obstacle in your journey.

> **「长夜月」：**成为你旅途上的一道阻碍，我很抱歉。

**"March 7th":** Don't apologize, Evernight.

> **「三月七」：**别道歉呀，「长夜月」。

**"March 7th":** We're just two sides reflected by the same mirror, aren't we?

> **「三月七」：**我们本就是同一面镜子映出的表里，对吧？

> **「三月七」：**所以，当我们告别分裂，合二为一……

> **三月七：**「记忆」只会变得更加美丽。

**March 7th:** And shout: "What took you so long to find me! Don't you know how to move faster?"

> **三月七：**我…真的好开心……

> **「长夜月」：**……

> **「长夜月」：**「新的生命若要萌芽，它的种子须是死的」……

**"Evernight":** March 7th... When you bid farewell to your past and are born from the Six-Phased Ice...

> **「长夜月」：**三月七，在你告别过去，从六相冰中诞生的时候……

**"Evernight":** Have you thought about the version of yourself you want to see in the mirror?

> **「长夜月」：**你想过，该映出一个怎样的自己吗？

> **「长夜月」：**「记忆」的命途，绝不止于世人表面的理解。而在这场破碎的阴谋中……

**"Evernight":** Will not allow you to become this broken conspiracy's next sacrifice.

> **「长夜月」：**我绝对不能，让你成为下一个牺牲品。

**"Evernight":** Then Trailblaze through the darkness in my stead... as always.

> **「长夜月」：**代我在黑暗中「开拓」，一如既往……

**"Evernight":** "Oblivion" shall keep watch over your past, veiled in the hush of the evernight, silent and serene. ♭

> **「长夜月」：**「忘却」会守望你的来路，如长夜般隐秘，永远安宁♭。

## 157　Gods, Sound the Anthem of Creation　诸神啊，奏响创世的凯歌

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Universal Matrix" Great Tomb of the Nameless Titan　|　角色：The Herta; Welt; Himeko; Sunday; Black Swan; Lygus; Anaxa; Irontomb; Dan Heng • Permansor Terrae; Trailblazer; March 7th; Cyrene; Aglaea; Castorice; Mydei; Phainon; Trianne; Trinnon; Tribbie; Cerydra; Hysilens; Hyacine; Cipher; Terravox; Mem; Evernight; Screwllum　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Reunion, Promise of Tears and Smiles'' completed

### Talk to the Astral Express crew

> *As the "final Era Nova" approaches, all factions must be prepared. For victory, we may need to seek more allies...*

**Herta:** Oh? The Chronocognitive Anchor readings...

> **黑塔：**喔？识刻锚的读数——

**Herta:** They actually pulled it off. Not too shabby at all.

> **黑塔：**他们还真成功了，真可以呀。

**Herta:** They got the First Genius, then they put the Garden in its place. This chapter in Trailblazing history may not be the last of its kind, but it's certainly the first of its kind!

> **黑塔：**先是第一位天才，然后又搞定了忆庭，这一站在「开拓」史上不说后无来者，也绝对称得上前无古人了吧？

**Himeko:** We couldn't have made it this far without the wholehearted support of the two geniuses.

> **姬子：**能走到这一步，离不开两位天才的倾囊相助。

**Welt:** ...And next, we'll be facing the Lord Ravager as they break free from their shell.

> **瓦尔特：**接下来，就要直面破壳而出的绝灭大君了。

**Herta:** Lemme give you a warning. The backdoor Screwy set up in the Era Nova system can only get you as far as breaching the core of the Scepter, nothing more.

> **黑塔：**我得事先提醒一句，螺丝在「再创世」进程里设置的后门，顶多帮你们攻入权杖内部。

**Herta:** As for those nastier malware programs lurking inside Irontomb's belly, you'll have to deal with them as they come.

> **黑塔：**至于铁墓肚子里那些更棘手的恶意程序，就只能见招拆招了。

**Himeko:** That's more than enough help to us. By the way, how are Mr. Screwllum's "preparations" coming along?

> **姬子：**这就足够了。说起来，螺丝先生的「准备」如何了？

**Herta:** Give him a bit more time. Those screwheads at the Imperial Advisory Council need to understand what the concept of "war" means first. For all we know, they might have already gutted all the fortresses in Screwy's collection while he was away.

> **黑塔：**再给他点时间吧，帝国咨政院的螺丝脑袋们要先理解什么是「战争」——搞不好，趁螺丝不在，那帮人早就把他藏库里的要塞全拆了。

**Welt:** I've heard Mr. Screwllum's fleet includes planet-class mechs: Hyacinth, Poppy, Oleander... Which one will answer the call, I wonder?

> **瓦尔特：**听说螺丝先生的藏品都是行星级。「风信子」、「虞美人」、「夹竹桃」…不知道会派遣哪一艘前来支援？

**Herta:** You sure know your stuff... I'd rather none of them show up and let my space station save some face.

> **黑塔：**真是如数家珍啊…我倒宁愿一艘也别来，给我的空间站留点面子。

**Black Swan:** The memory of this decisive battle is bound to be absolutely magnificent, isn't it?

> **黑天鹅：**这场决战的记忆，想来一定会壮丽无比吧？

**Black Swan:** Sorry to keep you waiting, everyone. I also have some news to share.

> **黑天鹅：**久等了，各位。这边也有一则消息——

**Black Swan:** The Xianzhou Alliance has replied that they are willing to offer what modest support they can in the campaign against Irontomb.

> **黑天鹅：**仙舟联盟已经回信，愿为征讨铁墓献上绵薄之力。

**Himeko:** The Alliance? Not just the Luofu? This is really good news. With this, our odds of victory just went up another notch.

> **姬子：**联盟…不止罗浮吗？这无疑是好消息。如此一来，胜算就又多了一分。

**Black Swan:** ...Whether this news is good or bad remains to be seen.

> **黑天鹅：**…好坏与否，恐怕还不得而知。

**Himeko:** What do you mean?

> **姬子：**什么意思？

**Black Swan:** Something has happened that even General Jing Yuan couldn't understand. He urgently wants me to inform the two geniuses.

> **黑天鹅：**发生了一件事，景元将军也百思不解。事态紧急，他希望我尽快转告两位天才——

**Black Swan:** That Amphoreus's causality has vanished from the Matrix of Prescience Ultima.

> **黑天鹅：**翁法罗斯的因果，从「大衍穷观阵」中消失了。

**Black Swan:** This means the "Instant" in Nous's calculations... is drawing closer to this world.

> **黑天鹅：**这意味着——博识尊计算中的「时刻」，正在向这个世界逼近。

### Talk to Zandar about the Demiurge

> *As the final Era Nova approaches, all factions must be prepared. Zandar was well aware of the Demiurge's existence...*

**"Zandar":** As the Theoros, I have observed...

> **「赞达尔」：**以神礼观众之名，我见到——

**"Zandar":** The night has faded away. And what I have created, I will destroy. This starry sky will return to freedom and chaos, just as it was in the beginning.

> **「赞达尔」：**夜晚已经散去——而后，我将摧毁我创造的一切，这片星空会重返自由和混沌，一如太初。

**Anaxa:** Confident, aren't you? But from where I stand, it seems you've run out of options.

> **那刻夏：**还真是自信啊。在我看来，你已经失去了所有手段。

**Anaxa:** When the Deliverer and his/her companions fully uncover the secret of "The 13th Titan"...

> **那刻夏：**等到救世主和他/她的伙伴彻底揭露那「第十三泰坦」的秘密……

**Anaxa:** That is the moment your schemes will come to ruin.

> **那刻夏：**便是你计划覆灭之时。

**"Zandar":** Lord Anaxagoras, I have granted access to all records concerning the "Demiurge" as per your request.

> **「赞达尔」：**阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下，我已应您要求，开放了所有关于「德谬歌」的记录。

**"Zandar":** How unfortunate that you'll never admit your own mistakes.

> **「赞达尔」：**多么遗憾，您永远不愿承认自己的谬误。

**"Zandar":** Analysis complete. Before I erase you completely, allow me to reveal the truth to you, if only as a courtesy to the final thinker...

> **「赞达尔」：**解析已经完成，在将您彻底抹消前，不妨由我告知真相，权当对最后一位智者的惺惺相惜……

**"Zandar":** The Demiurge never existed.

> **「赞达尔」：**德谬歌，它从未存在过。

**Anaxa:** If it never existed, then where did the nameless Titan's tomb come from?

> **那刻夏：**若它从未诞生，那无名泰坦大墓又是从何而来？

**"Zandar":** Isn't that exactly why it's called a tomb? Because it only houses the remains of what once was.

> **「赞达尔」：**陵墓之所以得名，不正是因为寄宿其中的——只是往日的遗骸么？

**"Zandar":** Let me give you an example from a story you're familiar with.

> **「赞达尔」：**就用您熟悉的故事举个例子吧。

**"Zandar":** A sage from the Grove once conducted an experiment. He took a chimera embryo, removed its head before maturity, and continuously stimulated its body to make the chimera believe it still had a brain, then nurtured it in an ichor vessel.

> **「赞达尔」：**某位树庭贤者曾做过实验：取一枚奇兽胚胎，在长成前摘下它的头颅，向其身体持续输入刺激，让奇兽相信自己仍有大脑，置于灵液匣中培育。

**"Zandar":** Remarkably, this chimera regrew its skull, but it was completely hollow inside. It had reserved a space for a brain it never possessed.

> **「赞达尔」：**奇妙的是，这只奇兽竟重新生出了颅骨，但空空如也——它为大脑留出了位置，却从未拥有过它。

**"Zandar":** The experiment was over. The sage had assumed this shell would perish once the stimulation ceased. Unfortunately, he was wrong.

> **「赞达尔」：**实验结束。贤者本以为这具躯壳在刺激停止后便会死亡。但很遗憾，他错了。

**"Zandar":** Driven by instincts, the chimera's body... seized the sage's head.

> **「赞达尔」：**在「本能」驱使下，奇兽的身体——夺取了贤者的头颅。

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** Ah. So that's it... I was stuck in a flawed line of thinking.

> **那刻夏：**原来，是我陷入了思维误区啊。

**"Zandar":** You've got it. I knew you'd understand.

> **「赞达尔」：**您果然理解了。

**"Zandar":** The 13th Titan never existed, but the Scepter must believe that "the Titan" exists.

> **「赞达尔」：**第十三位泰坦从未存在，但权杖必须相信「它」存在。

**"Zandar":** I killed that Titan with my own hands. That life form called "Demiurge" was excluded from the extrapolations from the very beginning. This is the only way to make Irontomb complete.

> **「赞达尔」：**是我亲手扼杀了它。那名为「德谬歌」的生命形态，从最初就被剔除在了演算之外。唯有如此，铁墓才能真正完成——

**"Zandar":** Yes. Irontomb is a headless giant, and to become whole, instinct will drive it to seize another head.

> **「赞达尔」：**没错。铁墓是一尊无首的巨人，要成为完整的生命，本能将驱使它夺取另一颗头颅——

**"Zandar":** ...Nous, the Erudition.

> **「赞达尔」：**——「智识」博识尊。

**Anaxa:** Your Titan... the Aeon of Erudition...

> **那刻夏：**属于你们的泰坦，「智识」的星神……

**Anaxa:** I see. So you were afraid, after all. Afraid of history repeating itself, of losing control over your creation yet again. You had to resort to turning it into a mere puppet.

> **那刻夏：**原来如此，你还是害怕了——害怕重蹈覆辙，自己的造物再度失去控制，只能用这种方式，把它变成一具傀儡。

**"Zandar":** I shall shackle god with my own hands. Irontomb will connect to THEIR body, infiltrate THEIR mind, and guide all THEIR extrapolations towards the Destruction...

> **「赞达尔」：**以我之手，为神明戴上枷锁。铁墓将接入祂的身躯，侵入祂的思想，将祂演算的一切导向「毁灭」……

**"Zandar":** I hear it: the tolling of the doomsday bell. After thirteen heartbeats, my first and final thesis shall converge at the end of the universe

> **「赞达尔」：**我听见——末日的钟声已经响起。十三次心跳后，我最初和最后的课题，将在宇宙的终点合一。

**"Zandar":** As for why I buried the "Demiurge" in a corner of my memory... Oh, regrettably, the answer wasn't about fear.

> **「赞达尔」：**至于我为何要将「德谬歌」尘封在记忆的角落，很遗憾，答案并非出于恐惧……

**"Zandar":** I simply forgot long ago. Nothing more.

> **「赞达尔」：**我早已遗忘了它，仅此而已。

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** Hah, hahahaha!

> **那刻夏：**哈、哈哈哈哈哈！

**"Zandar":** Your laughter is but a helpless sigh.

> **「赞达尔」：**阁下的笑声，已是无奈的喟叹。

**Anaxa:** Helpless? What a joke.

> **那刻夏：**无奈？别开玩笑——

**Anaxa:** You merely created a false god... but Amphoreus has long since written deicide into its fate.

> **那刻夏：**你不过是创造了一尊伪神，而翁法罗斯——早已将弑神写入了命运。

**Anaxa:** The final "Era Nova" is at hand. Why don't you wait and see?

> **那刻夏：**最后的「再创世」在即，不妨拭目以待……

**Anaxa:** The Deliverer, myself, and this world will together prove that the original Nouspore, the ultimate truth of the universe, is anything but Destruction.

> **那刻夏：**救世主，我，还有这个世界——会亲自证明，最初的智种，宇宙的终极，绝非「毁灭」。

**"Zandar":** See you in the next life, sage. If there's even a one-in-ten-thousand chance your conjecture is correct...

> **「赞达尔」：**来世见，智者。若你的猜想有万分之一的可能……

**"Zandar":** Then, in the name of "genius", I shall most certainly witness its failure.

> **「赞达尔」：**以「天才」之名，我定会见证它的失败。

### Return the Coreflame and complete the final "Era Nova"

> *Amphoreus! I do not beckon your name in haste. I come here to tell a tale of history. When the black tide of doom descended upon Earth, gods fell into madness, and mortals turned against each other. Yet there were Flame-Chasing heroes, pioneering the great enterprise of creating a world. In this world, they shall meld burning golden blood into their bodies. Will the destiny of days to come remember their names? In the name of Worldbearing, I promise you: Kephale will never forget.*

**Cyrene:** "All shall bid farewell to one, and that person alone will witness the miracle. Such is the will of fate."

> **昔涟：**「众人将与一人离别，惟其人将觐见奇迹，此乃命运使然。」

**Cyrene:** But this time... it will be the final Flame-Chase Journey...

> **昔涟：**然而这一次，也是最后一次逐火之旅……

**Cyrene:** The epic with endless cycles is about to end.

> **昔涟：**无限轮回的史诗，要迎来尾声了呀。

**Dan Heng:** The extrapolations of the Erudition, the Coreflames of the Destruction, and the textures of the Remembrance... the Three Paths converge once again at the world's end.

> **丹恒：**「智识」的演算、「毁灭」的火种、「记忆」的质料，三重命途在世界的尽头再度交汇。

**Dan Heng:** And "the Trailblaze" will write a new beginning for this world.

> **丹恒：**而「开拓」——会为它写下新的起点。

**March 7th:** Not only that, we'll also carry the history of all thirty million recurrences into the new world with us.

> **三月七：**不仅如此，我们会带着全部三千万世的过往一同走向新世界。

**March 7th:** It wouldn't be an exaggeration to call it a miracle.

> **三月七：**这一切，说是奇迹也不为过。

**(Trailblazer):** This miracle wasn't created by just one person.

> **开拓者：**这不是某一个人创造的奇迹。

**(Trailblazer):** Phainon would have long been consumed by the Coreflames if he hadn't chiseled his companions' dying wishes into his soul... And if the heroes' resolve to chase the flames had wavered, Cyrene's journey would never have begun...

> **开拓者：**若非将同伴的夙愿刻骨铭心，白厄早已被火种焚烧殆尽；如果逐火的信念产生动摇，昔涟的旅途也无从谈起……

**(Trailblazer):** The heroes never yielded and never fell during Amphoreus's thirty million cycles...

> **开拓者：**翁法罗斯三千万世，英雄们从未屈服，也从未倒下……

**(Trailblazer):** It was "fate" that fell instead.

> **开拓者：**——倒下的，是「命运」。

- ▸ Come. Let us complete the final Era Nova.
- ▸ Let us march toward the final battle against the Destruction.
- ▸ Let us carry the memory of thirty million recurrences into the future.
- ▸ Let us end this never-ending cycle.

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** I know you're watching, aren't you?

> **昔涟：**我知道，你在看着，对吗？

**Cyrene:** The original "PhiLia."

> **昔涟：**最初的「PhiLia」。

**???:** ...

> **？？？：**……

**Cyrene:** Weird... I still couldn't recover the memories of these thirty million lives in the end.

> **昔涟：**好奇怪呀，到最后，我也没能找回这三千万世的记忆。

**Cyrene:** But that doesn't matter anymore, right? I will make the same choice as you because that's just who I am, and I will always have my charm.

> **昔涟：**但那不重要了，对不对？我会做出和你一样的选择，因为人家的魅力就是始终如一。

**Cyrene:** Losses are a constant on the Flame-Chase journey, but even if everything fades away with the wind, there's one feeling that will remain...

> **昔涟：**逐火是不断失却的旅途，可就算一切随风逝去，也有一种感情会被留下来……

**Cyrene:** "Love" will always exist, right?

> **昔涟：**「爱」会永远存在，对吗？

**???:** ...

> **？？？：**……

**???:** Let's write an ending unlike any before for this world we so deeply love♪

> **？？？：**为这个我们深爱的世界，写下不同以往的结局吧♪

**Cyrene:** Behold, O gods! Amphoreus has achieved her victory, and the Era Nova approaches.

> **昔涟：**众神啊，看哪！翁法罗斯已完成了她的胜利，再创世即将到来——

**Cyrene:** The radiant soul has arrived, having walked through the bustling nights, bearing the golden fire and blood, stepping with pride into daylight.

> **昔涟：**那辉煌的灵魂已临到此地，行走过熙熙攘攘的黑夜；他/她携来黄金的火与血，胜利地步入白昼——

**(Trailblazer):** Amphoreus! I do not call your name suddenly or without cause. I come here to recount history.

> **开拓者：**翁法罗斯！我并非陡然呼唤你的名字。我来此，是为了讲述历史——

**(Trailblazer):** In this world, they fused burning golden ichor into their bodies.

> **开拓者：**此世，他们将燃烧的金血熔进身躯。

**(Trailblazer):** Will destiny remember their names in days to come?

> **开拓者：**来日的命运，可会记得他们的姓名？

**Tribios:** Isn't that gonna be way too many names to remember?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**那要记住的名字，是不是有点太多啦！

**Tribios:** Thirty million times a thousand... No, wait, times one thousand and one...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**三千万乘以一千，不对，是一千零一……

**Tribios:** Oh, let's not get hung up on that now. If there are more of us, then there'll be more "passages" in Amphoreus too!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**好啦，现在别纠结这个了。如果*我们*变多了，那翁法罗斯的「门径」也会变得更多！

**Tribios:** Together, let's reach the end of the west wind where flowers bloom in spring. See you tomorrow!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**一同抵达鲜花芬芳的西风尽头——明天见！

**Cerydra:** Now, the people of this world will no longer be bound by a single rule, but will make their own "Laws."

> **刻律德菈：**如此一来，世人不必再受唯一法则制约，而要做自己的「律法」。

**Cerydra:** Though the Imperator's figure may seem rather small against the stars beyond, she will suffice as the cornerstone of the world and become the foundation of the greatest empire.

> **刻律德菈：**凯撒的身躯在群星面前略显矮小，但足以成为世界的基石——成就最伟大的帝国。

**Hysilens:** Looks like this will be a feast that never comes to a close.

> **海瑟音：**看来，会是一场永不落幕的欢宴呢。

**Hysilens:** May the fish never leave the "Ocean", and may the days ahead... shine like pearls.

> **海瑟音：**愿鱼儿从不离开「海洋」，愿来日…如明珠般璀璨。

**Hyacine:** May the gentle "Sky" watch over all beings. There'll be no more distinctions of status or rank... only you, me, and everyone's shared hopes.

> **风堇：**让温柔的「天空」呵护世间。不再分贵贱高低，只有你、我，还有大家共同的心愿。

**Anaxa:** "Reason" or defects, they will all be sown by my own hands. If there's anything else to say, I urge future scholars not to put me on a pedestal. Just make use of what I leave behind.

> **那刻夏：**无论「理性」，还是缺陷，皆由我亲手种下。如果还要说什么——劝来世学者别把我捧上神坛，物尽其用吧。

**Aglaea:** May the newborn dawn keep the world's eyes ever bright. Though defiance may be bitter, "Romance" shall never perish.

> **阿格莱雅：**祝那新生的黎明，令世界目光常亮。抗争或许苦涩，但「浪漫」从不灭亡。

**Mydei:** In the name of the son of Gorgo: Warriors shall fight for "Strife," not with glory as their end, but to march forward in its light.

> **万敌：**以歌耳戈之子的名义：勇士将为「纷争」而战，不以荣耀为终点，而在光荣中前行。

**Castorice:** Even though "Death" shall eventually separate us, let every embrace we share be without regret.

> **遐蝶：**即便「死亡」终将分离你我，也请让每一次相拥不留遗憾。

**Cipher:** Why're you guys all so serious? Fiiine. Then I'll wish for there always to be water in deserts and gold in fertile soil, and for everything in this mortal realm to be attainable through "Trickery"!

> **赛飞儿：**都这么正经？那我就许愿沙漠永远有水，好土永远有黄金，人间一切都能用「诡计」换来吧！

**Dan Heng:** "Earth" shall protect the past, present, and future, eternally sustaining life.

> **丹恒：**「大地」会拱卫往世、此世与来世，生生不息——

**March 7th:** I-Is it my turn? Then let "Time" watch over the past and guide us to the future.

> **三月七：**轮、轮到我了吗？那，就让「岁月」守望过去，指引未来——

**(Trailblazer):** Just like the Trailblaze.

> **开拓者：**就像「开拓」。

**Phainon:** It isn't like you to be this serious, (Trailblazer).

> **白厄：**这么严肃可不像你啊，开拓者。

**Phainon:** Are you hesitating about how to carry out the divine authority of "Worldbearing"? Or do you already know the answer in your heart?

> **白厄：**是在犹豫怎么履行「负世」的神权么？还是说，你心里早就有答案了？

**Phainon:** That's right. Just keep moving forward. In your own way.

> **白厄：**没错，只要前进就好。用你自己的方式——

**Phainon:** Bring a true dawn to Amphoreus.

> **白厄：**为翁法罗斯，带来真正的黎明吧。

**(Trailblazer):** Then in the name of "Worldbearing," I promise you: Kephale will never forget this.

> **开拓者：**以「负世」之名，我向你保证：刻法勒永志不忘。

**(Trailblazer):** And now, let us all embark on this final grand journey.

> **开拓者：**至此，让我们所有人踏上最后的伟大征程。

**(Trailblazer):** Stand with me, and become heroes!

> **开拓者：**与我一同，成为英雄吧！
